### [77] [State-Averaged Quantum Algorithms for Multiconfigurational Surface Chemistry: A Benchmark on Rh@TiO2(110)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17925)
*Ernst Dennis Lægteskov Binau Larsson,Erik Kjellgren,Peter Reinholdt,Jacob Kongsted*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文评估了两种量子算法（SA-fUCCSD和SA-ADAPT）在Rh掺杂TiO2(110)表面NO吸附多态催化系统中的性能，发现自适应算法能以更少参数达到接近CASSCF的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表面催化过程建模需要处理强关联、电荷转移和多重电子态的方法。密度泛函理论对局域电子态有限制，而量子计算算法在化学多态系统中的性能尚未充分探索。

Method: 使用Rh掺杂TiO2(110)表面NO吸附的嵌入团簇模型作为测试系统，该系统具有显著的多构型特征和多重态交叉。以状态平均CASSCF为参考，评估SA-fUCCSD和SA-ADAPT作为固定轨道基下CASCI问题的求解器性能。

Result: SA-fUCCSD随电路深度增加而改善，但需要大量参数且对初始化敏感。SA-ADAPT能以显著更少的算子达到接近CASSCF的精度。改进的算子选择方案（每次迭代包含多个算子）大幅加速收敛。

Conclusion: 自适应ansätze在多态问题中表现出高效性，为量子算法在超越最小模型的化学系统中的应用建立了受控基准。

Abstract: Accurate modeling of surface catalytic processes often requires methods capable of describing strong correlation, charge transfer, and multiple closely lying electronic states. While density functional theory remains widely used, its limitations for localized electronic states motivate the use of wavefunction-based approaches and, more recently, quantum computing algorithms. However, the performance of quantum ansätze in chemically motivated, multistate settings remains largely unexplored.
  Here, we benchmark state-averaged factorized unitary coupled cluster with singles and doubles (SA-fUCCSD) and the adaptive, problem-tailored ansatz (SA-ADAPT) using an embedded cluster model of NO adsorption on Rh-doped TiO2(110). The system exhibits pronounced multiconfigurational character and multiple state crossings, providing a stringent test. State-averaged CASSCF serves as a reference, and the quantum ansätze are evaluated as solvers for the corresponding CASCI problem within a fixed orbital basis.
  We find that SA-fUCCSD improves with increasing circuit depth but requires many parameters and shows sensitivity to initialization. In contrast, SA-ADAPT achieves near-CASSCF accuracy with significantly fewer operators. A modified operator selection scheme, incorporating multiple operators per iteration, substantially accelerates convergence.
  Our results demonstrate the efficiency of adaptive ansätze for multistate problems and establish a controlled benchmark for quantum algorithms in chemically motivated systems beyond minimal models.

</details>


### [78] [Exponential quantum space advantage for Shannon entropy estimation in data streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18014)
*Weijun Feng,Yongzhen Xu,Lvzhou Li,Gongde Guo,Song Lin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 量子流算法在香农熵估计中实现指数级空间优势，对数空间复杂度对比经典算法的多项式空间需求


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有限量子比特的近量子设备在数据流模型中的空间受限量子计算，探索量子计算在流式处理中的优势

Method: 开发基于显式构造oracle的两阶段量子流算法，oracle从流输入中构建，实现对数空间复杂度

Result: 量子算法在精度参数上实现对数空间复杂度，而任何经典流算法都需要多项式空间，展示指数级量子空间优势

Conclusion: 香农熵估计在量子流模型中存在指数级空间分离，揭示了量子查询复杂度和流空间复杂度之间的根本差距

Abstract: Near-term quantum devices with limited qubits motivate the study of space-bounded quantum computation in the data stream model. We show that Shannon entropy estimation exhibits an exponential separation between quantum and classical space complexity in this setting. Technically, we develop a two-stage quantum streaming algorithm based on a quantum procedure with an explicitly constructed oracle derived from the streaming input. This algorithm achieves logarithmic space complexity in the accuracy parameter, whereas any classical streaming algorithm requires polynomial space. In sharp contrast, existing results for Shannon entropy estimation in the quantum query model achieve only a quadratic speedup. Our work establishes a natural problem with practical applications in computer networking that admits an exponential quantum space advantage, revealing a fundamental gap between quantum query complexity and streaming space complexity.

</details>


### [79] [Decoherence in Waveguide Quantum Electrodynamics using Matrix Product States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18036)
*Matias Bundgaard-Nielsen,Matthew Kozma,Sofia Arranz Regidor,Stephen Hughes*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种基于矩阵乘积态的波导量子电动力学密度矩阵方法，用于包含退相干过程，通过碰撞量子光学将波导多体态表示为离散时间仓，并推广到密度矩阵以包含Lindblad耗散项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在波导量子电动力学系统中，实际器件存在各种退相干过程（如纯退相位、辐射衰减等），这些过程对光-物质相互作用有重要影响。现有矩阵乘积态方法主要处理纯态演化，需要扩展到密度矩阵形式以包含耗散效应。

Method: 基于碰撞量子光学框架，将波导多体态表示为离散时间仓的矩阵乘积态链。将方法推广到密度矩阵表示，通过Liouvillian超算符中的Lindblad项包含各种损耗过程。该方法能够处理纯退相位、离片辐射衰减等多种耗散机制。

Result: 成功应用于多个波导量子电动力学系统：1) 半无限波导中具有时延反馈的两能级系统；2) 具有有限延迟的空间分离两能级系统；3) 少光子Fock脉冲在两能级系统上的散射。展示了纯退相位对光-物质相互作用的显著影响，并区分了纯退相位与离片辐射衰减的定性差异。

Conclusion: 该方法为波导量子电动力学中的耗散过程提供了高效的计算框架，能够处理实际系统中的关键退相干机制，为量子光子器件的设计和分析提供了重要工具。

Abstract: We present a matrix product state (MPS) method for including decoherence processes in calculations involving waveguide quantum electrodynamics (waveguide QED) using density matrices. The approach is based on collision quantum optics, where the many-body state of the waveguide is represented as discrete time bins, which are efficiently represented using an MPS chain. Our method is a generalization of previous MPS methods, and we demonstrate how one can efficiently expand to density matrices, allowing for the inclusion of various loss processes in the form of Lindblad terms in the Liouvillian superoperator responsible for the relevant dissipation dynamics. As an application of the theory, we study various waveguide QED systems and the influence of emitter pure dephasing (which is one of the most important processes in real systems) on the light-matter interactions, including a two-level system (TLS) in a semi-infinite waveguide with time-delayed feedback, two spatially separated TLSs with finite delays, and finally the scattering of few-photon Fock pulses on a TLS. In addition to emitter pure dephasing, we also show how to include off-chip radiative decay, and show how it differs qualitatively from pure dephasing.

</details>


### [80] [Shannon and Rényi entropies of molecular densities: insights into extensivity and the incomplete description of electron correlation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18081)
*Diogo J. L. Rodrigues,Evelio Francisco,Ángel Martín Pendás*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 电子密度和形状函数的信息论度量（Shannon和Rényi熵）作为电子相关描述符存在缺陷：无法准确捕捉静态相关，且部分违反广延性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究基于电子密度和形状函数的信息论度量（Shannon和Rényi熵）作为电子相关描述符的可靠性，特别关注这些熵度量是否能够准确编码静态相关信息并满足广延性要求。

Method: 通过建立熵度量的加性和非加性贡献的严格分解（基于Mulliken类原子划分），在无限核间距极限下系统分析熵的渐近行为，评估静态相关和广延性问题。结合代数分析和数值计算，在不同基组（最小基组和更大基组）和理论水平下进行系统研究。

Result: 1. 在最小基组和不同理论水平下，Shannon和Rényi熵无法编码波函数传递的静态相关量；2. 形状函数Shannon熵和Rényi熵（α≠1）违反广延性；3. 在更大基组中，非相关的Hartree-Fock密度始终高估熵值（相比充分相关的全价CAS密度）；4. 相关不足方法的熵违反广延性。

Conclusion: 基于电子密度的度量不足以捕捉静态相关，稳健的熵描述符应从更高维的希尔伯特空间对象构建。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the reliability of information-theoretic measures based on the electron-density and shape-function, specifically Shannon and Rényi entropies, as descriptors of electronic correlation. By establishing a rigorous decomposition of these entropic measures into additive and nonadditive contributions, supported on a Mulliken-like atomic partition of molecules, we systematically analyze the asymptotic behavior of the entropies at the infinite-internuclear-distance limit to assess the problem of static correlation and extensivity. Our algebraic and numerical analysis reveals several flaws in the use of these density-based descriptors. We demonstrate that for minimal-basis and different theoretical levels, the Shannon and Rényi entropies fail to encode the amount of static correlation conveyed by the underlying wavefunction. Conversely, shape-function Shannon entropies and Rényi entropies (for $α\neq 1$) violate extensivity. In larger basis sets, uncorrelated Hartree-Fock densities consistently overestimate entropy compared to sufficiently correlated (e.g., full-valence-CAS) densities. Moreover, the entropies for insufficiently correlated methods violate extensivity. These findings indicate that electron-density-based measures are insufficient for capturing static correlation, suggesting that robust entropic descriptors should be constructed from higher-dimensional Hilbert-space objects.

</details>


### [81] [Generation of energy-time entangled triphotons in a six-level cold atomic system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18110)
*Ling Niu,Zhiyin Duan,Na Liu,Yitong Zhai,Shaoyan Liu,Junsheng Li,Donghai Zhang,Da Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在六能级冷原子系综中研究能量-时间纠缠三光子的产生机制，揭示其受严格时序约束的特性，并证明条件双光子态的时间关联特性得以保留，为W类三体纠缠提供了关键支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前在热原子系综中观察到的能量-时间纠缠三光子受限于复杂的五阶非线性磁化率（涉及四或五能级系统），限制了对其产生机制的理解。需要更直接地捕捉三光子的产生机制及其光学特性。

Method: 研究六能级冷原子系综中能量-时间纠缠三光子的产生。通过分析系统的五阶非线性磁化率，揭示系统中存在两组自发六波混频过程。特别关注三光子产生所受到的严格时序约束。

Result: 三光子产生受严格时序约束，导致三重符合计数在二维时域中表现出不对称阻尼拉比振荡，且这些振荡主要由五阶非线性磁化率主导。分析推导表明，条件双光子态的时间关联特性得以保留，这是W类三体纠缠的独特特征。

Conclusion: 该研究不仅为使用六能级系统实验制备三光子奠定了基础，还为理解涉及更复杂五阶非线性磁化率的三光子产生机制提供了关键支持，揭示了W类三体纠缠的独特时间关联特性。

Abstract: Multiphoton entangled states are pivotal resources for implementing optical quantum information protocols. Recently, energy-time-entangled triphotons have been observed in hot atomic ensembles. However, in these protocols, the complex fifth-order nonlinear susceptibility entailed by four- or five-level systems limits our understanding of triphoton generation. Here, to directly capture the generation mechanism of triphotons and their associated optical properties, we investigate the generation of energy-time-entangled triphotons in a six-level cold atomic ensemble. The fifth-order nonlinear susceptibility indicates the existence of two sets of spontaneous six-wave mixing in the system. Notably, triphoton generation in this system is subject to stringent timing constraints. Collectively, these characteristics give rise to threefold coincidence counts, which -- dominated by the fifth-order nonlinear susceptibility -- exhibit asymmetrically damped Rabi oscillations in the two-dimensional time domain. Furthermore, we analytically derive that the temporal correlation properties of conditional two-photon states are preserved -- a unique feature of $W$-class tripartite entanglement. These results not only lay the groundwork for the experimental preparation of triphotons using six-level systems but also provide key support for understanding the generation mechanism of triphotons involving more complex fifth-order nonlinear susceptibilities.

</details>


### [82] [Dissipative dynamics and superradiant countinuous time crystal in a Rydberg-dressed Dicke system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18119)
*Haohang Zhou,Xianfeng Chen,Luqi Yuan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究探索了包含里德堡修饰相互作用的开放Dicke模型，揭示了其非平衡动力学，发现了新的临界耦合和动力学相变，并证明了超辐射连续时间晶体相的存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究多体相互作用与受控耗散之间的相互作用，为探索非平衡量子相提供了丰富框架。通过结合驱动耗散量子腔和相互作用原子系统，构建一个实验可行的平台来研究动力学相变和宏观时间序。

Method: 采用包含里德堡修饰相互作用的开放Dicke模型，在驱动耗散腔中研究其非平衡动力学。超越平均场极限，分析系统支持的超辐射连续时间晶体相。

Result: 里德堡修饰相互作用产生额外的临界耦合，改变不动点的稳定性并决定丰富的动力学相变。系统支持超辐射连续时间晶体相，证明CTC可以存在于相互作用的spin-1/2系统中。通过腔发射光子提供可测量的特征。

Conclusion: 里德堡修饰的Dicke系统为探索开放量子物质中的动力学相变和宏观时间序提供了一个多功能且实验可行的平台，通过腔发射光子提供可测量的特征。

Abstract: The interplay between many-body interactions and controlled dissipation provides a rich framework for exploring nonequilibrium quantum phases. In this work, we explore an open Dicke model including Rydberg-dressed interactions in a driven-dissipative cavity and unveil its unique nonequilibrium dynamics therein. We find that Rydberg-dressed interactions generate an additional critical coupling, which alters the stability of fixed points and hence determines fruitful dynamical phase transitions. Beyond the mean-field limit, we demonstrate that our system supports a superradiant continuous time crystal (CTC) phase, proving CTC can exist in an interacting spin-1/2 system. By bridging driven-dissipative quantum cavity and interacting atomic systems, our Rydberg-dressed Dicke system offers measurable signatures from the cavity emission photons, making it experimentally feasible as a versatile platform for exploring dynamical phase transitions and macroscopic temporal order in open quantum matter.

</details>

<details>

Result: 建立了超导混合量子机电系统的统一框架，详细阐述了量子比特-机械耦合的物理机制，包括相位和电荷自由度相互作用产生的纵向和横向耦合。展示了如何将这些平台扩展到电光机械架构，实现超导电路与光学光子的相干接口，为传感器应用提供了理论基础。

Conclusion: 超导混合量子机电系统为量子传感和混合量子系统提供了强大的平台。通过理解量子比特-机械耦合机制及其扩展到电光机械架构，可以开发新型量子传感器和接口技术，推动超导量子技术在传感领域的应用发展。

Abstract: Superconducting qubits, realized by incorporating Josephson junctions into superconducting circuits, behave as artificial atoms with anharmonic energy spectra and can be precisely controlled and measured using microwave cavities within the framework of circuit quantum electrodynamics (cQED). Since its emergence in the early 2000s, cQED has established superconducting qubits as leading candidates for scalable quantum devices and has enabled the exploration of hybrid quantum systems that integrate disparate physical platformsThis review surveys superconducting hybrid quantum electromechanical systems in which mechanical resonators are coupled to superconducting qubits, with a focus on two widely used qubit platforms: the transmon and the fluxonium. We provide an overview of the underlying coupling mechanisms arising from interactions through the phase and charge degrees of freedom of the qubit, and discuss how these mechanisms give rise to both longitudinal and transverse qubit-mechanical interactions. We further review extensions of electromechanical platforms to electro-optomechanical architectures, in which optical cavities are integrated to enable coherent interfacing between superconducting circuits and optical photons. This review aims to present a unified framework and perspective on qubit-mechanical and qubit-mechanical-optical hybrid systems in superconducting quantum technologies and applications related to sensors.

</details>


### [84] [Quantangle-SAT: A Quantum SAT Solver Based on Entanglement and Equivalence Checking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18218)
*Shang-Wei Lin,Ji-Qing Yan,Yean-Ru Chen,Zhe Hou,David Sanán*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种基于纠缠和等价性检查的新型量子SAT求解器，无需已知解的数量，比量子计数更高效，在随机布尔函数上期望时间复杂度为O(1)


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Grover算法的量子SAT求解器需要预先知道布尔公式满足解的数量，这在大多数实际场景中不可得。虽然量子计数可以估计解的数量，但计算开销比Grover搜索高几个数量级。

Method: 提出基于纠缠和等价性检查的新型量子SAT求解方法。该方法不假设预先知道解的数量，通过量子纠缠和等价性检查机制来高效解决SAT问题。

Result: 虽然最坏情况时间复杂度仍是指数级的，但证明了在随机布尔函数上期望时间复杂度仅为常数时间O(1)。实验结果支持了理论主张。

Conclusion: 该方法解决了现有量子SAT求解器需要预先知道解数量的关键限制，提供了一种更实用的量子SAT求解方案，在随机布尔函数上具有常数期望时间复杂度。

Abstract: Satisfiability (SAT) is a central problem in computer science, and advances in SAT-solving algorithms have a far-reaching impact across many fields. Recent works have proposed quantum SAT solvers based on Grover's algorithm, a quantum search technique. However, Grover-based approaches face a key limitation: they typically require prior knowledge of the number of satisfying assignments of the target Boolean formula. This information is unavailable in most practical settings. Quantum counting can be used to estimate this quantity, but it incurs a computational overhead that is several orders of magnitude higher than Grover search. In this paper, we propose a novel quantum SAT solver based on entanglement and equivalence checking. Our method does not assume prior knowledge of the number of solutions and is computationally more efficient than quantum counting. Although the worst case time complexity is inevitably exponential, we prove that the expected time complexity of our approach is only constant time O(1) over random Boolean functions. Experimental results also support our theoretical claim.

</details>


### [85] [Equivalence of Local Dynamical Hidden-Variable Models to Static Bell Locality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18238)
*Ming Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文证明任何严格的局部动力学模型在数学上等价于静态贝尔模型，无法绕过贝尔定理


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理直觉认为局部动力学演化或测量引起的扰动可能绕过贝尔定理，但需要严格证明这种可能性是否存在

Method: 建立广义转移核框架，涵盖任意局部动力学，严格证明任何严格局部动力学模型在数学上等价于静态贝尔模型

Result: 证明试图绕过这种等价性的尝试（包括历史时间模型和最近的宏观导波流体动力学模拟）必然引入显式非定域性或放弃测量独立性

Conclusion: 建立了普遍边界：非定域统计相关性不能由纯粹的局部动力学过程产生

Abstract: Physical intuition suggests that local dynamical evolution or measurement-induced disturbances of hidden variables might bypass Bell's theorem. We establish a generalized transition-kernel framework encompassing arbitrary local dynamics and rigorously prove that any strictly local dynamical model is mathematically equivalent to a static Bell model. We show that attempts to circumvent this equivalence -- including historical temporal models and recent macroscopic pilot-wave hydrodynamic analogs -- inevitably introduce explicit nonlocality (parameter dependence) or forfeit measurement independence. Our results establish a general boundary: nonlocal statistical correlations cannot be generated by purely local dynamical processes.

</details>


### [86] [Block-encodings as programming abstractions: The Eclipse Qrisp BlockEncoding Interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18276)
*Matic Petrič,René Zander*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Eclipse Qrisp框架中的BlockEncoding接口，将块编码作为高级编程抽象，简化了量子算法实现和资源估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 块编码是现代量子算法的基础技术，但生成可编译的实现具有挑战性。需要为更广泛的科学受众提供高级编程抽象，简化块编码的构建和应用。

Method: 在Eclipse Qrisp框架中开发BlockEncoding接口，包含构造函数、核心工具、算术组合和算法应用。通过代码示例展示接口如何抽象块编码构造、量子化等底层概念。

Result: 建立了块编码作为高级编程抽象，简化了矩阵求逆、多项式滤波和哈密顿模拟等量子算法的实现。接口既提供了技术框架介绍，也提供了实践教程。

Conclusion: BlockEncoding接口使块编码技术更易于访问，促进了高级量子算法如QSVT、QSP和CKS算法的实际实现和资源估计。

Abstract: Block-encoding is a foundational technique in modern quantum algorithms, enabling the implementation of non-unitary operations by embedding them into larger unitary matrices. While theoretically powerful and essential for advanced protocols like Quantum Singular Value Transformation (QSVT) and Quantum Signal Processing (QSP), the generation of compilable implementations of block-encodings poses a formidable challenge. This work presents the BlockEncoding interface within the Eclipse Qrisp framework, establishing block-encodings as a high-level programming abstraction accessible to a broad scientific audience. Serving as both a technical framework introduction and a hands-on tutorial, this paper explicitly details key underlying concepts abstracted away by the interface, such as block-encoding construction and qubitization, and their practical integration into methods like the Childs-Kothari-Somma (CKS) algorithm. We outline the interface's software architecture, encompassing constructors, core utilities, arithmetic composition, and algorithmic applications such as matrix inversion, polynomial filtering, and Hamiltonian simulation. Through code examples, we demonstrate how this interface simplifies both the practical realization of advanced quantum algorithms and their associated resource estimation.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用半经典平均场描述和完全量子理论，推导二阶相干函数的解析表达式；提出单离子声子激光新方案；探索压缩基和不同Lamb-Dicke近似区域中的激光行为；分析基于压缩态的传感协议。

Result: 理论分析确认了实验观测的激光行为阈值特性；提出的单离子声子激光方案具有显著实验优势；压缩基中的激光产生能够产生非经典态；基于压缩态的传感协议在实验可行参数下可实现高达两个数量级的灵敏度提升。

Conclusion: 该研究为声子激光提供了全面的理论框架，提出的单离子方案简化了实验实现，展示了声子激光在产生非经典态和精密传感中的巨大应用潜力，为量子技术发展提供了新方向。

Abstract: In this article we present a comprehensive theoretical investigation of phonon lasing with mixed-species trapped ions, as demonstrated in [T. Behrle, Phys. Rev. Lett. 131 (2023)], employing both a semi-classical mean-field description and a full quantum theory. We derive an analytic expression for the second-order coherence function, confirming the experimental observation of the system's lasing behaviour above threshold. Building on the successful implementation of the two-ion lasing scheme, we propose a novel approach for achieving phonon lasing with a single trapped ion, offering significant experimental advantages and making the implementation of multiple phonon lasers within a single setup feasible. Furthermore, we explore lasing in a squeezed basis and in different regimes of the Lamb-Dicke approximation, highlighting the potential to produce non-classical states with promising applications in precision sensing. Our analysis of a sensing protocol based on squeezed states, using experimentally feasible parameters, shows a sensitivity enhancement of up to two orders of magnitude.

</details>

Method: 作者提出了一个完整表征所有完美量子策略的方法，通过建立共享态和测量算子的必要和充分条件。该方法识别了任何完美策略必须满足的结构性约束集合，从而为理解这些游戏提供了统一框架。

Conclusion: 该工作为量子魔术矩形游戏建立了统一的完美量子策略表征框架，揭示了量子非局域策略的结构性约束，为理解量子关联提供了新的理论工具和见解。

Abstract: Quantum magic rectangular games provide a natural setting for studying perfect quantum strategies and their underlying structure. However, existing analyses are largely case-specific and do not reveal a unifying characterization. In this work, we give a complete characterization of all perfect quantum strategies for quantum magic rectangular games, in the form of necessary and sufficient conditions on the shared state and measurement operators. Our approach identifies a structural set of constraints that any perfect strategy must satisfy, thereby providing a unified framework for understanding these games. As an illustration, our characterization shows that even in the $3 \times 3$ case, two pairs of bell states are not structurally enforced for perfect quantum strategies. More broadly, our results offer new insights into the structure of quantum correlations underlying perfect nonlocal strategies.

</details>

Abstract: We experimentally demonstrate the detection of momentum transfers from individual collisions of Kr, Xe, and SF$_6$ with an optically levitated nanoparticle, finding good agreement with theoretical expectations. The observed event rates accurately measure the gas partial pressures, while the spectral shape provides a sensitive probe of the surface properties of the nanoparticle, including its temperature. The reconstruction of impulse signals as small as 200 keV/$c$ further establishes that levitated optomechanical sensors can reach the sensitivity required for precision measurements of fundamental particle interactions, and demonstrates a proof-of-principle for a primary pressure sensor based on the detection of individual gas particle collisions.

*Samuel D. Escribano,Yael Kriheli,Samuel Goldstein,Daniel Dahan,Nadav Katz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于共享Π型滤波器的宽带Purcell保护方案，通过微波干涉抑制环境导纳实部，实现多量子比特同时保护，Purcell限制弛豫时间超过1ms，带宽约1.5GHz


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Purcell保护方案通常针对单个量子比特或需要复杂硬件，难以在紧凑架构中同时保护多个量子比特。需要一种宽带、紧凑且硬件开销最小的解决方案，兼容标准色散读取协议。

Method: 设计Π型滤波器（两个开路短截线通过传输线连接），直接集成到馈线中。利用工程化的被动微波干涉抑制环境导纳实部。通过电路仿真和有限元建模验证性能，在目标频带（量子比特频率）内强烈抑制传输，同时保留多路复用架构的读取和重置模式。

Result: 对于实际器件参数，该设计在约1.5GHz频率跨度内实现Purcell限制弛豫时间超过1ms。滤波器在目标频带内强烈抑制传输，同时保持读取和重置模式功能。性能可通过设计修改进一步扩展。

Conclusion: Π型滤波器为超导量子电路中的宽带阻抗工程提供了紧凑、可扩展的解决方案，兼容标准色散读取协议，能够同时保护多个量子比特，硬件开销最小。

Abstract: We propose a broadband Purcell-protection scheme based on a single shared filter integrated directly into the feedline, enabling simultaneous protection of multiple qubits in a compact architecture with minimal hardware overhead. The filter consists of two open-ended stubs connected by an in-line transmission line, forming a $Π$ geometry, and operates via engineered passive microwave interference that suppresses the real part of the environmental admittance over a wide frequency window. Circuit simulations and finite-element modeling show strong suppression of transmission within the target band (the qubit's frequencies) while preserving the readout and reset modes of the multiplexed architecture. For realistic device parameters, the proposed design yields Purcell-limited relaxation times exceeding $1$ ms over a frequency span of approximately $1.5$ GHz, which can be further extended with straightforward modifications of the design. Our results establish the $Π$-filter as a compact and scalable solution for broadband impedance engineering in superconducting quantum circuits, compatible with standard dispersive readout protocols.

</details>


### [91] [Security Risks of VOA-Induced Luminescence in Chip-Based quantum key distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18422)
*Zijian Li,Chenyu Xu,Xin Hua,Yongqiang Du,Xin Liu,Tao Lin,Xi Xiao,Kejin Wei*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文首次系统研究了集成QKD发射机中p-n结可变光衰减器的实现级安全漏洞，发现其电偏置时会产生自发发光，形成波长分辨侧信道，可能导致信息泄露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管集成光子学被视为可扩展量子密钥分发的关键使能技术，但集成光子组件的实现安全性尚未得到充分理解。需要研究p-n结可变光衰减器这一普遍组件可能存在的安全漏洞。

Method: 采用理论和实验相结合的方法，使用单光子敏感光谱测量技术分析p-n结VOA的电偏置自发发光特性，测量发射波长，并将测量结果纳入定量安全分析。

Result: 实验发现p-n结VOA在电偏置时会产生中心波长约1107 nm的自发发光，与C波段量子信号光谱分离，形成波长分辨侧信道。定量分析表明即使极弱的发射也会导致不可忽视的信息泄露。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了光子集成QKD系统中一个基本且先前被忽视的安全风险，强调了未来集成量子通信技术需要安全感知的设备设计。

Abstract: Integrated photonics is widely regarded as a key enabler for scalable quantum key distribution (QKD), offering compactness, stability, and compatibility with semiconductor fabrication. Despite rapid advances in chip-based QKD, the implementation security of integrated photonic components remains insufficiently understood. Here we present the first systematic study of an implementation-level security vulnerability associated with p-n junction-based variable optical attenuators (VOAs), a ubiquitous component in integrated QKD transmitters. We theoretically and experimentally demonstrate that electrically biased p-n junction VOAs emit spontaneous luminescence. Using a single-photon-sensitive spectral measurement technique, we identify the emission wavelength to be centered around 1107 nm, well separated from the C-band quantum signals. This spectral separation gives rise to a previously unrecognized wavelength-resolved side channel, enabling potential wavelength-splitting attacks without directly disturbing the encoded quantum states. By incorporating the measured luminescence into a quantitative security analysis, we show that even extremely weak emission can lead to non-negligible information leakage. Our findings reveal a fundamental and previously overlooked security risk in photonic integrated QKD systems and highlight the necessity of security-aware device design for future integrated quantum communication technologies.

</details>


### [92] [Classical counterparts of shortcuts to adiabaticity in nonlinear dissipative Lagrangian systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18439)
*Jincheng Shi,Yicheng Pan,Yue Ban,Xi Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文将量子系统中的绝热捷径（STA）概念扩展到经典非线性耗散拉格朗日系统，通过r-θ机械臂模型展示了如何实现快速变换并抑制残余激励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将量子系统中发展起来的绝热捷径（STA）技术应用于经典非线性耗散系统，建立量子STA概念与经典对应物之间的实用桥梁，解决经典系统中快速变换时的残余能量和误差问题。

Method: 使用耦合r-θ机械臂作为示例模型，在具有Rayleigh耗散的欧拉-拉格朗日方程上进行逆向工程，通过规定端点静止轨迹来获得相应的力和扭矩分布；比较平滑STA协议与执行器有界时间最优解以及PID跟踪；引入基于单次中途测量的校正方法。

Result: 量化了几何耦合如何放大误差和残余能量；揭示了平滑性、速度和鲁棒性之间的权衡关系；单次中途测量校正方法能够减少早期偏差的影响，同时保持输入几乎平滑。

Conclusion: 成功将量子STA概念应用于经典非线性耗散拉格朗日系统，为量子STA概念与其经典对应物之间建立了实用桥梁，提供了在经典系统中实现快速变换并抑制残余激励的有效方法。

Abstract: Shortcuts to adiabaticity (STA) were first developed in quantum dynamics to realize rapid transformations with suppressed residual excitations. Here we show how the same idea can be implemented in classical nonlinear dissipative Lagrangian systems. Using a coupled $r$-$θ$ manipulator as an illustrative model, we perform inverse engineering on the Euler-Lagrange equations with Rayleigh dissipation by prescribing endpoint-stationary trajectories, obtaining the corresponding force and torque profiles and quantifying how geometric coupling amplifies errors and residual energy. We further compare smooth STA protocols with actuator-bounded time-optimal solutions and with proportional-integral-derivative tracking, which highlights a trade-off among smoothness, speed, and robustness. Finally, we introduce a single-shot correction based on one mid-course measurement to reduce the effect of early deviations while keeping the inputs nearly smooth. These results provide a practical bridge between quantum STA concepts and their classical counterparts.

</details>


### [93] [Recurrence analysis of quantum many-body dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18446)
*Tomasz Szołdra,Matheus S. Palmero,Peter Schmelcher*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 将经典非线性时间序列分析中的递归分析方法引入量子多体系统，用于表征非平衡量子动力学，特别是通过递归图和递归量化分析检测量子相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平衡量子多体系统的可观测量表现出复杂的时间行为，这些行为编码了底层物理机制，但通常难以直接解释。需要开发新的分析框架来理解这些复杂动力学。

Method: 引入递归分析——一种在经典动力系统中长期建立的非线性时间序列分析框架。使用递归图提供模拟或实验数据的定性指纹，同时通过递归量化分析提取相应的数值描述符。将该框架应用于一维横场伊辛模型中从顺磁基态的淬火过程。

Result: 在双点关联的递归图中观察到清晰的进展：在深度铁磁相中观察到近乎周期性的模式，在临界点处转变为多尺度时间结构。递归量化指标无需先验模型知识即可恢复临界场强。

Conclusion: 递归分析是表征量子多体动力学的通用工具，包括量子相变的无监督检测。该方法为理解复杂量子动力学提供了新的分析视角。

Abstract: Observables of out-of-equilibrium quantum many-body systems display complex temporal behavior that encodes the underlying physical mechanisms but typically resists straightforward interpretations. We introduce recurrence analysis - a nonlinear time-series analysis framework long established for classical dynamical systems - to investigate correlated quantum many-body dynamics. Recurrence plots provide a qualitative fingerprint of simulated or experimental data, while recurrence quantification analysis extracts corresponding numerical descriptors. Applying this framework to quenches from the paramagnetic ground state in the one-dimensional transverse-field Ising model, we observe a clear progression in the recurrence plots of two-site correlations: nearly periodic patterns in the deeply ferromagnetic phase give way to multiscale temporal structures at criticality. Recurrence quantifiers further recover the critical field strength without prior knowledge of the model, establishing recurrence analysis as a versatile tool for characterizing quantum many-body dynamics, including unsupervised detection of quantum phase transitions.

</details>


### [94] [Disorder-induced non-Gaussian states in large ensembles of cavity-coupled molecules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18456)
*R. Schwengelbeck,M. Pandini,R. Daraban,J. Schachenmayer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在极化子化学的集体电子强耦合玩具模型中，无序性导致振动模式在单分子层面产生非高斯态，且该效应在大分子体系中保持鲁棒性，核波包无法用热态有效描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究极化子化学中腔修饰核动力学的无序性和量子效应的重要性，探索振动动力学在集体电子强耦合下的行为。

Method: 使用Holstein-Tavis-Cummings模型，通过精确矩阵乘积态模拟分析无序性对振动动力学的影响，并与Ehrenfest近似和截断Wigner近似等半经典方法进行比较。

Result: 无序性导致振动模式在短时间尺度上产生非高斯态，该效应在大分子体系中保持鲁棒性；Ehrenfest近似仅在大系统尺寸下能较好再现系综平均可观测量，截断Wigner近似无法捕捉非高斯效应。

Conclusion: 无序性和真正的量子效应在极化子化学的腔修饰核动力学中具有关键重要性，核波包无法用热态有效描述，需要精确量子方法而非半经典近似。

Abstract: We analyze vibrational dynamics in a toy model for polaritonic chemistry under collective electronic strong coupling. In a Holstein-Tavis-Cummings model, incoherently excited by a photon, we show that disorder leads to non-Gaussian states of vibrational modes on short time scales at the single-molecule level. Using exact matrix product state simulations, we demonstrate that this effect can remain robust for larger molecule numbers, implying that nuclear wave packets cannot be effectively described by thermal states. Furthermore, we compare simulations of the exact quantum dynamics with semiclassical approximations. We find that the Ehrenfest approximation can only well reproduce ensemble-averaged observables for very large system sizes. Also simulations in the truncated Wigner approximation fail to capture the non-Gaussian effects. Our work highlights the importance of disorder and genuine quantum effects in cavity-modified nuclear dynamics in polaritonic chemistry.

</details>


### [95] [Scaling of Quantum Resources for Simulating a Long-Range System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18495)
*Tanya Keshari,Debasis Sadhukhan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 使用VQE模拟一维长程扩展伊辛模型，研究量子资源随系统规模和相互作用强度的缩放关系。构建了三种结构感知的ansatz，引入对数负性作为寻找基态的新判据，发现相互作用范围参数α主要决定所需ansatz层数的最小值，而非量子临界点的接近程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究量子模拟中资源缩放问题，特别是对于长程相互作用系统。传统基于能量保真度的方法在寻找基态时存在不足，需要更可靠的判据。同时需要理解相互作用范围如何影响量子模拟的资源需求。

Method: 采用变分量子本征求解器(VQE)模拟一维长程扩展伊辛模型。构建了三种结构感知的ansatz：最近邻(NN)、次近邻(NNN)和次次近邻(NNNN)纠缠块，模仿哈密顿量中的弦算符。引入基于成对对数负性的新判据来可靠地寻找基态。

Result: 1) 能量保真度单独不是寻找模型基态的良好指标；2) 相互作用范围参数α主要决定所需ansatz层数的最小值，而非量子临界点的接近程度；3) 在非局域区域(α≤1)，NNN和NNNN ansatz相对于NN分别将层缩放率降低了2.5倍和3.8倍；4) 可靠模拟所需的两量子比特门总数随系统规模呈二次增长(所有三种ansatz)；5) 在局域区域，所需两量子比特门数量线性增长；6) 在准局域区域，所需两量子比特门数量更复杂，取决于哈密顿量的相。

Conclusion: 通过结构感知的ansatz设计和基于对数负性的新判据，成功分析了长程伊辛模型的量子模拟资源缩放。相互作用范围参数α是决定资源需求的关键因素，而非临界性。在非局域区域，更长的相互作用ansatz能显著减少所需层数，但两量子比特门总数仍呈二次增长，与哈密顿量中非局域项的理论预测一致。

Abstract: We simulate a long-range extended Ising model in one dimension using a hybrid quantum algorithm, namely Variational Quantum Eigensolver (VQE). In this quantum simulation, we investigate how quantum resources scale with system size and interaction strength. Three structure-aware ansatze incorporating nearest-neighbor (NN), next-nearest-neighbor (NNN), and next-next-nearest-neighbor (NNNN) entangling blocks are constructed by mimicking the string operators in the Hamiltonian. We show that energy fidelity alone is not a good indicator for finding the ground state of our model. To overcome this problem, we introduce an additional criterion based on pairwise logarithmic negativity as a more reliable way to find the actual ground state by the VQE. We find that the interaction range parameter alpha primarily governs the minimum number of ansatz layers required, rather than proximity to the quantum critical point. In particular, we show that in the non-local regime (alpha <= 1), the NNN and NNNN ansatze reduce the layer scaling rate by factors of 2.5x and 3.8x relative to NN in all phases, including the critical point. The total number of two-qubit gates required for reliable simulation grows quadratically with system size for all three ansatze. This is consistent with the theoretical prediction, as the number of non-local terms in the Hamiltonian also grows quadratically with the system size. In the local regime, however, the number of required two-qubit gates grows linearly with system size. In contrast, in the quasi-local regime, the required number of two-qubit gates for the quantum simulation is more subtle and depends on the phase of the Hamiltonian.

</details>


### [96] [AtomTwin.jl: a physics-native digital twin framework for neutral-atom quantum processors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18531)
*Shannon Whitlock*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: AtomTwin.jl是一个开源的Julia软件包，用于开发、模拟中性原子量子处理器及相关原子量子设备的量子协议、硬件配置和构建数字孪生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前量子计算领域需要能够连接数学模型与物理设备的工具，特别是在中性原子量子处理器领域。现有工具通常需要用户手动定义哈密顿量，缺乏从物理几何和参数直接建模的能力，限制了硬件级协议开发和数字孪生构建的效率。

Method: 开发了AtomTwin.jl软件包，能够从物理几何和参数直接建模原子、光镊、激光场、原子运动、相互作用和噪声过程，无需用户手动定义哈密顿量。软件包提供硬件级指令序列、量子与经典耦合动力学的高性能求解器，以及镱-171原子的即用模型，采用可扩展框架设计以支持更多原子种类和硬件组件。

Result: 软件架构设计完成，性能基准测试显示优于现有工具箱。通过端到端应用演示验证了软件功能：在可移动光镊中使用四个镱-171原子制备了[[4,2,2]]错误检测码的逻辑贝尔态。

Conclusion: AtomTwin.jl为中性原子量子处理器提供了一个强大的开发、模拟和数字孪生平台，通过直接从物理参数建模简化了量子协议开发流程，其可扩展架构为未来支持更多原子种类和硬件组件奠定了基础。

Abstract: AtomTwin.jl is an open-source Julia package for developing and simulating quantum protocols, hardware configurations and building digital twins for neutral-atom quantum processors and related atomic quantum devices. AtomTwin operates between mathematical models and physical devices; modeling atoms, optical tweezers, laser fields, atomic motion, interactions, and noise processes natively from physical geometry and parameters, without requiring users to define Hamiltonians manually. The package provides hardware-level instruction sequences, high-performance solvers for coupled quantum and classical dynamics, and a ready-to-use model for ytterbium-171 atoms in an extensible framework designed to accommodate a greater variety of atomic species and hardware components in the future. This paper describes the software architecture, performance benchmarks against existing toolboxes, and a demonstrated end-to-end application: preparation of a logical Bell state in the $[[4,2,2]]$ error-detecting code with four $^{171}$Yb atoms in moveable tweezers.

</details>


### [97] [Hamiltonian dynamics from pure dissipation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18533)
*Zhong-Xia Shang,Daniel Stilck França*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 纯耗散动力学可以模拟哈密顿动力学，通过零哈密顿项但非零迹的跳变算符的Lindblad主方程，以O(t²/ε)的时间代价实现ε精度的逼近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索开放量子系统中纯耗散动力学能否模拟封闭系统的哈密顿演化，揭示耗散与相干动力学之间的基本关系。

Method: 使用GKSL表示，构建零哈密顿项但非零迹跳变算符的Lindblad主方程，通过纯耗散生成元近似哈密顿动力学，分析时间缩放和几何约束。

Result: 证明了纯耗散动力学能以O(t²/ε)的时间代价在钻石范数下ε近似哈密顿动力学，该缩放是最优的，并导出了BQP完备性、Zeno冻结效应、无超二次加速定理等推论。

Conclusion: 哈密顿动力学可通过纯耗散过程有效模拟，揭示了耗散追赶相干速度的基本退相干代价，为Lindbladian模拟和量子计算提供了新视角。

Abstract: The fundamental difference between closed and open quantum dynamics lies in their environmental interaction: closed systems are perfectly isolated and evolve reversibly under unitary Hamiltonian dynamics, whereas open systems continuously couple to an external bath, resulting in irreversible dissipation and information loss. In this work, we show internal Hamiltonian dynamics can be "faked`` via external pure dissipation, i.e., Lindbladians without a coherent Hamiltonian part. More concretely, we show that, in a GKSL representation with zero explicit Hamiltonian term but nontraceless jump operators, bounded-norm dissipative generators can approximate Hamiltonian dynamics within $ε$ error in diamond norm using $\mathcal{O}(t^2/ε)$ evolution time. We further prove that for time-independent dynamics this $\mathcal{O}(t^2/ε)$ scaling is in the worst case, necessary and optimal from a geometric perspective, which captures the fundamental decoherence cost for catching up with the speed of Hamiltonian dynamics. Our construction leads to various implications, including the BQP-completeness of purely dissipative dynamics even before reaching approximate equilibrium, a Zeno-adjacent state-independent freezing effect, the no super-quadratic fast-forwarding theorem of a class of purely dissipative dynamics, and reducing Lindbladian simulation cost via gauge changing.

</details>


### [98] [Dissipative Preparation of Correlated Quantum States in Dipolar Rydberg Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18542)
*Mingsheng Tian,Zhen Bi,Thomas Iadecola,Bryce Gadway*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种基于耗散辅助原子的非互易能量选择性跃迁协议，用于将偶极量子系统引导至目标多体态，无需哈密顿量先验知识且可稳定整个能谱中的态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 制备关联量子态对新兴技术至关重要，但在多体系统中仍然具有挑战性。现有方法通常局限于基态制备或需要哈密顿量先验知识。

Method: 引入两种可控耗散辅助原子作为非互易的激发和退激发通道，实现希尔伯特空间中的定向行走。该方法特别设计用于偶极里德堡原子阵列，但适用于具有类似能力的系统。

Result: 该方法能够稳定多体能谱中的任意态（不限于基态），无需哈密顿量先验知识，为可编程平台提供了灵活且可扩展的态制备框架。

Conclusion: 提出的耗散协议通过非互易能量选择性跃迁，为多体量子系统的态制备提供了一种通用、灵活且可扩展的方法，特别适用于偶极里德堡原子阵列等可编程平台。

Abstract: Preparing correlated quantum states is essential for emerging technologies, but remains challenging in many-body systems. Here we propose a dissipative protocol that engineers nonreciprocal, energy-selective transitions to steer dipolar quantum systems toward desired many-body states. This is realized by introducing two types of controllable dissipative auxiliary atoms that act as nonreciprocal excitation and de-excitation channels, respectively, enabling a directional walk in Hilbert space. This approach enables stabilization of states across the many-body spectrum, not limited to the ground state and requiring no \textit{a priori} knowledge of the Hamiltonian. Our approach is designed for neutral atoms in dipolar Rydberg arrays, but applies broadly to setups with similar capabilities, providing a flexible and scalable framework for state preparation in programmable platforms.

</details>
