### [99] [Unveiling Topological Fusion in Quantum Hall Systems from Microscopic Principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16525)
*Arkadiusz Bochniak,Shinsei Ryu,Jürgen Fuchs,Gerardo Ortiz*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出一个组合框架，直接从微观数据推导分数量子霍尔流体中任意子准粒子的融合规则，基于波函数在最低朗道能级的占据模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立分数量子霍尔流体中任意子准粒子的融合规则对于理解其底层拓扑序至关重要。关键拓扑性质被编码在候选多体波函数的"DNA"中，即限制在有限个最低朗道能级的主导轨道占据模式。

Method: 扩展Schrieffer计数论证，引入拓扑激发类别，提出一个组合框架，直接从微观数据（波函数在最低朗道能级的占据模式）推导融合规则。

Result: 该框架为阿贝尔和非阿贝尔激发提供了统一的融合规则推导路径，阐明了拓扑特征从第一原理在费米子和玻色子系统中的涌现。

Conclusion: 提出的组合框架能够直接从微观波函数数据推导任意子的融合规则，为理解分数量子霍尔系统的拓扑序提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: Establishing the fusion rules of anyonic quasiparticles in fractional quantum Hall fluids is essential for understanding their underlying topological order. Building on the conjecture that key topological properties are encoded in the "DNA" of candidate many-body wave functions - that is, the pattern of dominant orbital occupations restricted to a finite number of lowest Landau levels - we propose a combinatorial framework that derives these fusion rules directly from microscopic data. By extending Schrieffer's counting argument and introducing classes of topological excitations, our framework provides a unified route to the fusion rules for both Abelian and non-Abelian excitations. This approach elucidates the emergence of topological features from first principles in both fermionic and bosonic systems.

</details>


### [100] [Nonequilibrium Cooper quartet generation in superconducting devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16647)
*Luca Chirolli,Alessandro Braggio,Michele Governale*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种在双量子点系统中隔离和检测Cooper四重态的方法，通过非平衡驱动实现真空态与四电子态之间的共振，并观察到与四重态耦合相关的特征性输运信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cooper四重态作为Cooper对的推广，可以揭示关联物质和多体物理中未被探索的视角。然而，在实验上隔离和检测这种四电子集体态具有挑战性，需要开发新的平台和方法来研究四重态关联和多费米子关联态。

Method: 提出在双量子点系统中，通过耦合常规超导引线和正常引线，并驱动系统远离平衡态。在高偏压条件下，实现真空态|0⟩与四电子态|4e⟩之间的共振，该过程涉及两个Cooper对的交换。通过调节额外超导引线的相位来调控四重态耦合强度Γ_{4e}，并分析系统的输运特性、电流-电流关联和Fano因子。

Result: 在高偏压条件下观察到Andreev电流的峰值，其宽度与四重态耦合强度Γ_{4e}成比例缩放。通过电流-电流关联和Fano因子分析，发现了一个具有相等自关联和交叉关联的区域，这被解释为量子点与超导引线之间快速相干双Cooper对振荡的明确特征。四重态耦合可以通过额外超导引线的相位进行调控。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种实验上可实现的固态量子平台，能够隔离和探测Cooper四重态关联。通过输运测量和关联分析，可以明确识别四电子集体态的特征，为探索四重态关联和多费米子关联物质态开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Cooper quartets are aggregates of four electrons that generalize the concept of Cooper pairs, and their study can unfold unexplored perspectives in correlated matter and many-body physics. We propose a method to isolate them in a double-quantum-dot system coupled to conventional superconducting and normal leads. By driving the system out of equilibrium, we show that a resonance between the vacuum $|0\rangle$ and the four-electron state $|4e\rangle$ emerges in the high bias voltage regime, which involves a two-Cooper pair exchange process and is characterized by finite quartet correlations. We study the transport properties of the system and show that a peak in the Andreev current at high bias voltage has a width that scales with the magnitude of the quartet coupling $Γ_{4e}$, which can be tuned by the phase of additional superconducting leads, yielding distinctive signatures. By further studying the current-current correlations and the Fano factor, we establish a regime characterized by equal auto- and cross-correlations, which we interpret as a definitive signature of fast coherent two-Cooper-pair oscillations between the dots and the superconducting leads. The proposed platform, experimentally accessible in a quantum solid-state laboratory, enables exploration of quartet correlations and multifermion-correlated states of matter.

</details>


### [101] [Anisotropic spin-valley coupling in SiMOS and Si/SiGe quantum dots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16713)
*N. Tobias Jacobson,Natalie D. Foster,Ryan M. Jock,Andrew M. Mounce,Daniel R. Ward,Malcolm S. Carroll,Dwight R. Luhman*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究测量了SiMOS和Si/SiGe量子点中界面自旋轨道耦合的角度依赖性，建立了物理模型来推断谷内和谷间SOC，发现SiMOS的自旋-谷耦合比Si/SiGe大一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硅量子点中的自旋轨道耦合虽然比体硅强，但会影响电子自旋量子比特的性能。谷内和谷间SOC会扰动量子比特操作，而自旋-谷耦合在谷分裂与塞曼能量共振时会导致不良的自旋弛豫。需要理解不同硅材料平台（SiMOS和Si/SiGe）中SOC的特性差异。

Method: 测量SiMOS和Si/SiGe异质结构中界面自旋轨道相互作用随外加磁场方向和大小变化的角度依赖性。构建物理模型，通过数据拟合准确推断谷内和谷间SOC物理，直接比较两种材料系统。

Result: 测量器件显示，虽然g因子差异相当，但SiMOS量子点表现出比Si/SiGe大一个数量级的自旋-谷耦合。两种器件的自旋-谷耦合角度依赖性相似，具有相似的最小化自旋-谷耦合的磁场方向。

Conclusion: 该工作为优化自旋-谷耦合提供了操作方案，既可避免也可利用这一机制进行量子比特操作。研究揭示了不同硅量子点平台中SOC特性的重要差异，对量子比特设计有指导意义。

Abstract: While bulk silicon has long been understood to exhibit relatively weak spin-orbit coupling (SOC), confinement of electrons to quantum dots (QDs) at a silicon heterointerface results in significantly larger SOC. This is a concern for electron spin qubit performance, as intravalley and intervalley SOC can significantly perturb the operation of electron spin qubits. While these interactions can be harnessed to drive coherent rotations in a singlet-triplet qubit, coupling to low-lying excited valley states can lead to undesirable spin relaxation when valley splitting is on resonance with the Zeeman energy. In this work, we measure the angular dependence of the interfacial spin-orbit interaction as a function of the direction and magnitude of an applied external magnetic field in SiMOS and Si/SiGe heterostructures, two common material platforms for silicon spin qubits. We construct a physical model that accurately infers intra- and inter-valley SOC physics from fits to the data, allowing for a direct comparison between these two material systems. For the devices measured we find that, while the $g$-factor differences are comparable, the SiMOS QDs exhibit an order of magnitude larger spin-valley coupling than for Si/SiGe. Moreover, we find that the angular dependence of the spin-valley coupling is similar for both devices, with similar magnetic field orientations minimizing the spin-valley coupling. Our work points towards operational schemes for optimizing spin-valley coupling to avoid or exploit this mechanism for qubit operation.

</details>


### [102] [Charge-Density Waves of Single and Double NbS$_{3}$ Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16837)
*S. Tanda,S. Kashimoto,H. Yamamoto,K. Inagaki,H. Nobukane,Y. Fukuda*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 首次在真正一维单链和双链NbS₃系统中观测到电荷密度波，单链出现(1/4)b* CDW而非体材料中的(1/3)b*，双链中(1/2)b*二聚结构与(1/3)b* CDW共存


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真正一维系统中电子态物理性质尚不明确，以往实验均在准一维体材料中进行，无法直接反映真正一维电子态。传统认为费米面嵌套驱动的电荷密度波会在一维电子-晶格系统中出现，但迄今观测到的CDW转变都发生在准一维系统中。

Method: 采用碳纳米管鞘方法研究孤立的单链和双链NbS₃样品，首次在真正一维系统中进行实验观测。

Result: 在单链中意外观测到(1/4)b* CDW，而非体材料中常见的(1/3)b* CDW；在双链中确认(1/2)b*二聚结构与(1/3)b* CDW共存。这些发现超越了以往体材料研究的局限性。

Conclusion: 首次在真正一维系统中发现CDW，单链和双链NbS₃表现出与体材料不同的CDW行为，这标志着低维物理领域的重大进展，揭示了真正一维电子态的特有性质。

Abstract: The physics of a genuine one-dimensional system in which electrons are confined in one direction remains unclear. The actual electronic state of such a genuinely one-dimensional system has not been investigated in previous experiments, for they have all been conducted on quasi-one-dimensional specimens, namely in strongly anisotropic bulk crystals. Conventionally, charge-density waves (CDWs) driven by Fermi surface nesting have been considered to appear in one-dimensional electron-lattice systems. However, the CDW transitions actually observed to date have all occurred in quasi-one-dimensional systems and therefore do not directly indicate a genuine one-dimensional electronic state.
  We investigated, for the first time, isolated single and double-chain NbS$_{3}$ samples using the carbon-nanotube-sheath method and discovered CDWs in both systems. In the single-chain, surprisingly, a $(1/4)b^*$ CDW was observed, in contrast to the $(1/3)b^*$ CDW that has been observed in bulk samples. In the double-chain, the coexistence of a $(1/2)b^*$ dimer structure and a $(1/3)b^*$ CDW was confirmed. This discovery represents a significant advancement in the field of low-dimensional physics, surpassing the limitations of previous studies on bulk systems.

</details>


### [103] [Evolution of topological phases in atomically thin WTe2 films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16860)
*Changcang Qiao,Chen-Chia Hsu,Tao Zhang,Zhiming Sun,Dong Qian,Yang-hao Chan,Peng Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究通过角分辨光电子能谱和第一性原理计算揭示了WTe2从单层到块体的拓扑相变，发现拓扑性质随厚度呈现非单调振荡变化，层间耦合导致拓扑Z2不变量在1和0之间振荡，最终演变为外尔半金属。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究拓扑材料（从拓扑绝缘体到半金属）中拓扑相变的机制，特别是维度变化如何影响拓扑性质，探索层间耦合在拓扑相变中的作用。

Method: 采用角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）测量WTe2从单层到块体的电子结构，结合第一性原理计算分析能带结构和拓扑性质变化。

Result: 单层WTe2存在能隙，三层时能隙消失变为金属性；拓扑Z2不变量随层数增加在1和0之间振荡；系统最终演变为外尔半金属，拓扑性质由陈数描述。

Conclusion: 拓扑态对维度具有非单调依赖性，层驱动的电子能带重构导致固体中的拓扑相变，层间耦合通过改变能带交叉点影响拓扑性质。

Abstract: Topological materials ranging from topological insulators to semimetals host many novel quantum phenomena including quantum spin Hall effect and topological Fermi arcs. Transitions between these topological phases have attracted much research interest. We performed angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES) on WTe2 ranging from a monolayer to the bulk and reveal the evolution of the electronic structure and the band gap. Notably, the gap observed in the monolayer system is suppressed in the three layers, where the film becomes metallic. Variations in the topological properties with thickness are demonstrated by the first-principles calculations. Topological Z2 invariant is shown to oscillate between 1 and 0 with the addition of layers, originating from the interlayer coupling-induced change in band crossing. The system evolves into a Weyl semimetal when the conduction and valence bands touch near the Fermi level and the topological nature is described by the Chern number. Our findings demonstrate the non-monotonic dependence of topological states on dimensionality and how layer-driven electronic band reconfiguration leads to phase transitions in solids.

</details>


### [104] [Wave Packet Propagation in Tilted Weyl Semimetals for Black Hole Analog Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16927)
*M. A. Lozande,E. A. Fajardo*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究展示了如何在倾斜外尔半金属中实现两种不同类型的模拟黑洞视界，通过分析波包动力学揭示了完全反射和可穿透两种视界行为，并发现零动量波包在视界区域经历最强的效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在凝聚态系统中实现模拟黑洞视界的新平台，研究量子效应和信息动力学与引力类似物之间的关联。

Method: 通过比较两种具有不同光谱特性的模型，利用空间变化的Weyl锥倾斜来模拟引力黑洞视界附近的光锥倾斜效应，分析波包动力学以揭示不同的视界行为。

Result: 发现了两种根本不同的模拟视界：第一种模型表现出完全波包反射（不可穿透屏障），第二种模型允许波包穿过视界。零动量波包在视界区域经历最强的效应（显著减速和更长的停留时间），且两个系统都表现出显著的概率损失，这与波包在视界附近的停留时间直接相关。

Conclusion: 倾斜外尔半金属为研究模拟黑洞视界相关的非平凡量子效应和信息动力学提供了一个丰富且可调控的平台。

Abstract: We explore the realization of distinct analog black hole horizons within tilted Weyl semimetals by comparing two models with contrasting spectral properties. We demonstrate that a spatially varying tilt in the Weyl cone structure creates an effect analogous to the tilting of light cones near a gravitational black hole horizon. By analyzing wave packet dynamics in both models, we reveal two fundamentally different types of analog horizons. The first model exhibits complete wave packet reflection, effectively mimicking an impenetrable barrier. In contrast, the second model permits wave packet transmission across the horizon. Critically, for both models, wave packets initialized with zero momentum ($k_0=0.0$) experience the strongest horizon effects, characterized by a dramatic slowing and significantly longer dwell times at the horizon region. Finally, we find that both systems exhibit substantial probability loss, which we demonstrate is directly correlated with the wave packet's dwell time near the horizon. Our findings establish tilted Weyl semimetals as a rich, tunable platform for investigating non-trivial quantum effects and information dynamics associated with analog black hole horizons.

</details>


### [105] [The origin and promise of transition metal dichalcogenide hosted single photon emitters for quantum technologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17033)
*Mayank Chhaperwal,Amartyaraj Kumar,Kausik Majumdar*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文对过渡金属二硫化物（TMDCs）中单光子发射器（SPEs）的原子起源进行了批判性综述，分析了现有相互矛盾的理论，提出了表征方法标准化建议，并评估了TMDC基SPEs在量子技术应用中的现状与挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TMDCs单层材料作为有前景的单光子发射器平台，其原子级起源存在高度争议和相互矛盾的理论。为了推动该领域发展，需要澄清SPEs在TMDCs中的起源机制，并建立标准化的表征方法以促进技术应用。

Method: 1. 对TMDCs中SPEs原子起源的现有理论进行批判性综述；2. 对TMDC基SPEs的关键性能指标进行趋势分析；3. 提出标准化的表征方法以规范报告流程；4. 综述固态SPEs在量子技术中的应用，并识别TMDC基SPEs在这些技术中所需的改进。

Result: 论文系统梳理了TMDCs中SPEs起源的争议性理论，分析了不同性能指标的发展趋势，提出了标准化的表征框架，并明确了TMDC基SPEs在量子技术应用中需要解决的关键挑战。

Conclusion: TMDCs作为单光子发射器平台具有巨大潜力，但需要澄清其原子起源机制、建立标准化表征方法，并解决在量子技术应用中面临的性能挑战，才能实现其在实际量子技术中的成功应用。

Abstract: Single photon emitters (SPEs) are integral parts of several quantum technology implementations. Over the past decade or so, monolayers of transition metal dichalcogenides (TMDCs) have emerged as one of the promising candidates for SPE platforms with attractive characteristics. To move ahead, it is necessary to understand the atomistic origin of SPEs in TMDCs - a topic which is highly debated with contradicting proposals. In this paper, we critically review these existing proposals to elucidate their origin. Further, we perform a critical trend analysis for different figures of merit of TMDC-based SPEs, and propose a characterization methodology to streamline the reporting process. Finally, we review several quantum technology implementations where solid state SPEs are being used, and identify the advancements required in TMDC-based SPEs for their successful adoption in these technologies.

</details>


### [106] [Polarized light Raman scattering by an atom near an ultrathin periodically aligned carbon nanotube film](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17246)
*SK Firoz Islam,Michael Dean Pugh,Igor V. Bondarev*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究了双能级原子系统在超薄周期性排列碳纳米管薄膜附近的拉曼散射效应，发现近场介质辅助增强效应可达10^4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究各向异性超表面（特别是周期性排列碳纳米管薄膜）对双能级原子系统拉曼散射的量子近场介质辅助增强效应，重点关注入射光偏振和入射面相对于纳米管排列轴的取向等参数。

Method: 建立理论模型，系统研究双能级原子系统在超薄介电薄膜（内含周期性排列单壁半导体碳纳米管阵列）附近的拉曼散射效应。推导拉曼散射截面公式，分析入射光偏振（p偏振和s偏振）和入射面相对于纳米管排列轴取向的影响。

Result: 对于碳纳米管超表面近场区域的双能级原子系统，拉曼散射效应可增强高达10^4倍，不仅适用于p偏振光，也适用于s偏振光。该模型为面内各向异性超表面的量子近场介质辅助增强效应提供了统一描述。

Conclusion: 周期性排列碳纳米管薄膜作为各向异性超表面的代表性示例，能显著增强双能级原子系统的拉曼散射效应，增强因子可达10^4倍，且对p偏振和s偏振光均有效，为量子近场增强应用提供了理论指导。

Abstract: We present a systematic theoretical study of the Raman scattering effect for a two-level atomic system in near proximity of an ultrathin dielectric film with an embedded parallel array of periodically aligned single-wall semiconducting carbon nanotubes. More generally, our model provides a unified description of the quantum near-field medium-assisted enhancement effects for in-plane anisotropic metasurfaces, of which ultrathin periodically aligned carbon nanotube films are the representative example. Particular attention is given to incoming photon parameters of the external light radiation such as polarization and incidence plane orientation relative to the main anisotropy axis (nanotube alignment axis). By explicitly deriving the Raman scattering cross-section, we establish that for the two-level atomic system in the near-field zone of the carbon nanotube metasurface the effect can be enhanced by a factor of up to 10^4, not only for p-polarized but for s-polarized light as well.

</details>


### [107] [Quantum higher-spin Hall insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17479)
*Takuto Kawakami,Igor Kuzmenko,Yshai Avishai,Yigal Meir,Masatoshi Sato*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文发展了任意自旋J的量子自旋霍尔绝缘体理论，发现系统支持J+1/2对螺旋边缘态，具有高阶色散和独特的非线性输运响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将量子自旋霍尔物理扩展到高自旋系统，探索超越传统自旋1/2体系的新奇拓扑相和边缘态特性。

Method: 发展了任意自旋J的量子自旋霍尔绝缘体理论框架，通过镜像陈数分析拓扑保护性，推导边缘态的高阶色散关系，研究外加磁场和磁畴壁对边缘谱的影响。

Result: 系统支持J+1/2对螺旋边缘态，由非平庸镜像陈数保护；边缘理论由高阶色散的广义狄拉克费米子描述；产生非线性电压输运响应；面内磁场打开边缘谱质量隙；磁畴壁处存在(J+1/2)重简并束缚态，具有非平庸绕数。

Conclusion: 该工作将量子自旋霍尔物理推广到高自旋系统，揭示了新的拓扑边缘态特性和输运行为，为在超冷原子气体中实现此类系统提供了理论指导。

Abstract: We develop a theory of quantum spin Hall insulators with arbitrary spin $J$. Our analysis demonstrates that such systems support $J+\tfrac{1}{2}$ pairs of helical edge modes protected by nontrivial mirror Chern numbers. We establish that the corresponding edge theory is described by a generalized Dirac fermion with higher-order dispersion. These modes produce unique transport responses that are non-linear with voltage. An in-plane magnetic field opens a mass gap in the edge spectrum, and magnetic domain walls host $(J+\tfrac{1}{2})$-fold degenerate bound states characterized by nontrivial winding numbers. Our results extend quantum spin Hall physics to higher-spin systems and suggest possible realizations in ultracold atomic gases.

</details>


### [108] [Photocurrent at oblique illumination and reconstruction of wavefront direction with 2d photodetectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17591)
*Kirill Kapralov,Vladislav Atlasov,Alina Khisameeva,Viacheslav Muravev,Weiwei Cai,Dmitry Svintsov*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 基于金属-2DES对称结的光电探测器实现了零偏压斜入射光电流，并能通过载流子密度变化重建光入射方向


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有光电探测器能重建光谱和偏振信息，但无法确定光入射方向。需要开发能重建光入射方向的新型光电探测器。

Method: 使用金属与二维电子系统（2DES）的对称结结构。利用金属接触2DES的特殊电动力学特性，将入射场相位变化转化为局部场振幅变化。通过测量不同载流子密度下的光电流来重建入射方向。

Result: 实现了斜入射下的零偏压光电流，光电流方向唯一确定光入射象限。在二维等离子体共振条件下，通过改变载流子密度可定量确定入射角度。

Conclusion: 金属-2DES对称结光电探测器首次实现了光入射方向的重建，为单像素级全光信息探测提供了新途径。

Abstract: Many contemporary photodetectors operate beyond the readout of light intensity and enable the reconstruction of spectrum and polarization at the single-pixel level. However, the determination of light incidence direction with reconstructive detectors has not been realized so far. We show that photodetectors based on symmetric junctions of metals and 2d electron systems (2DES) enable (1) zero-bias photocurrent at oblique light incidence (2) reconstruction of incidence direction based on photocurrent measurements at variable carrier density. The former effect is based on peculiar electrodynamics of metal-contacted 2DES, where spatial variations of incident field phase translate into strong variations of local field amplitude. The local absorbances at two opposite metal-2DES junctions at oblique incidence are dissimilar, which results in finite photocurrent independent of microscopic rectification mechanism at these junctions. The direction of photocurrent uniquely determines the quadrant of light incidence. Quantitative determination of incidence angle becomes possible under conditions of 2d plasmon resonance at variable carrier density. In such a case, obliquely incident radiation excites the asymmetric plasmon modes, which amplitude carries unique information about angle of incidence.

</details>


### [109] [Microscopic Theory of Acoustic Phonon Scattering by Charge-Density-Wave Fluctuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17655)
*Han Huang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文开发了一个格林函数理论，将电荷密度波（CDW）序与晶格动力学联系起来，统一解释了衍射、软模光谱和热输运实验数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CDW序在关联金属中源于电子响应在特定波矢的峰值，其前驱涨落会衰减声学声子，即使长程序不存在。现有理论需要统一解释衍射、软模光谱和热输运实验。

Method: 开发格林函数理论：使用阻尼谐振子传播子描述CDW-晶格混合软模，通过电子回路获得应变-强度顶点，计算声学声子自能。理论识别两个散射通道：局域强度通道（由延迟复合CDW响应控制）和纹理（梯度）通道（耦合声学应变与CDW包络空间变化）。

Result: 理论框架统一了衍射、软模光谱和热输运测量，识别了两个散射通道，建立了软模质量追踪恒等式，并成功应用于2H-TaSe₂的IXS声子软化和异常热输运实验。

Conclusion: 该理论框架广泛适用于多种CDW材料，包括过渡金属二硫化物、稀土三碲化物、kagome CDW化合物和铜氧化物涨落电荷序体系，为理解CDW材料中晶格动力学与电子序的耦合提供了统一描述。

Abstract: Charge-density-wave (CDW) order in correlated metals originates in a peaked electronic susceptibility at a finite wavevector $\mathbf Q_0$, set either by Fermi-surface features (nesting or saddle-point singularities) or by momentum-resolved electron-phonon coupling, or by a combination of the two. CDW precursor fluctuations can attenuate heat-carrying acoustic phonons even when long-range order is absent. We develop a Green's-function theory in which a damped-harmonic-oscillator propagator for a hybrid CDW--lattice soft mode at the ordering wavevector $\mathbf Q_0$ and a strain--intensity vertex obtained from an electron loop combine to give the acoustic phonon self-energy. The theory identifies two scattering channels: a local-intensity channel, controlled by a retarded composite CDW response and giving a narrow critical contribution when the CDW correlation length is large, and a texture (gradient) channel, which couples acoustic strain to spatial variations of the CDW envelope and, in a frozen-texture limit, reduces to a phenomenological form set by the measured diffraction peak weight and width. The same propagator fixes the lattice projection of a hybrid CDW--phonon soft pole measured by inelastic X-ray scattering, with an underdamped-to-overdamped crossover controlled by the distance to the CDW instability and a mass-tracking identity for the slow overdamped relaxation rate. The framework unifies diffraction, soft-mode spectroscopy, and thermal transport and applies broadly across CDW materials, including the transition-metal dichalcogenides, rare-earth tritellurides, kagome CDW compounds, and the cuprate fluctuating charge-order regime; we illustrate it by direct comparison with experimental IXS phonon softening and anomalous thermal transport in 2H-TaSe$_2$ at elevated temperatures.

</details>


### [110] [Magnetoresistance from decoherence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17672)
*Xian-Peng Zhang,Yan-Qing Feng,Haiwen Liu,Yugui Yao*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了一种新的磁阻机制，源于整个费米海的量子退相干，而非传统的动量弛豫，其电导率与杂质密度呈线性关系，与传统的Drude模型相反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统磁阻理论主要关注动量弛豫和巡游电子的等离子体频率，但本文旨在探索量子退相干作为磁阻起源的新机制，为理解磁输运现象提供新视角。

Method: 提出基于密度矩阵非对角分量衰减的量子退相干理论，参数化两个复退相干时间，分析电导率与杂质密度的标度关系，并研究外磁场与交换场的相互作用。

Result: 发现电导率与杂质密度呈线性标度关系（与传统Drude模型的倒数关系相反），观察到温度驱动的正负磁阻交叉转变，以及类似Kondo效应的非单调温度依赖性和电导率最大值。

Conclusion: 量子退相干是磁阻的关键成分，这一发现为磁阻研究提供了新的理论框架和实验探针，对基础研究和纳米技术应用具有重要影响。

Abstract: Microscopic theories of magnetoresistance have traditionally focused on momentum relaxation and the plasma frequency of itinerant electrons. Here, we uncover a distinct mechanism in which magnetoresistance originates from quantum decoherence throughout the whole Fermi sea, specifically the decay of the off-diagonal components of the density matrix. The resulting conductivity, parameterized by two complex decoherence times, scales linearly with impurity density-markedly contrasting the conventional Drude picture, where conductivity is governed by momentum relaxation of Ferm-surface quasiparticles and is inversely proportional to impurity density. This unconventional scaling provides a direct electrical probe of quantum decoherence, a quantity central to both fundamental studies and emerging nanoscale technologies. Furthermore, the interplay between the external magnetic field and the exchange field gives rise to rich magnetotransport phenomena, including temperature-drive crossover from positive to negative magnetoresistance and a nonmonotonic temperature dependence with a conductivity maximum reminiscent of the Kondo effect. Our results establish quantum decoherence as a key ingredient in magnetoresistance and our findings should have an unprecedented impact on advancing research and applications involving magnetoresistance.

</details>


### [111] [Dynamical spin-nematic order in a transverse field Ising chain with non-Hermitian Gamma interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17700)
*Yu-Hong Yan,Ran Wang,Kun-Liang Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 研究非厄米Gamma相互作用对横场伊辛链相变和磁关联的影响，发现除了有能隙的铁磁和顺磁相外，还存在无能隙相，其中系统因宇称-时间对称性破缺而表现出长程自旋向列序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非厄米Gamma相互作用与伊辛相互作用、横场竞争所产生的新奇量子相，特别是宇称-时间对称性破缺如何诱导自旋向列序，为非平衡动力学表征相图提供方案。

Method: 研究横场伊辛链中的非厄米Gamma相互作用，分析宇称-时间对称性破缺对相变和磁关联的影响，通过非平衡动力学方法表征自旋向列相图。

Result: 发现系统存在三个相：有能隙铁磁相、有能隙顺磁相和无能隙相。在无能隙相中，宇称-时间对称性破缺诱导出长程自旋向列序，并揭示了动力学自旋向列序的出现，为通过非平衡动力学表征相图提供了途径。

Conclusion: 非厄米Gamma相互作用与伊辛相互作用、横场的竞争产生丰富的量子相，特别是宇称-时间对称性破缺诱导的自旋向列序，为在自旋链中生成自旋向列序提供了方案，并展示了非平衡动力学在相图表征中的应用。

Abstract: We investigate the effect of non-Hermitian Gamma interaction on the phase transitions and magnetic correlations for the transverse field Ising chain. We demonstrate that apart from the gapped ferromagnetic and paramagnetic phases, there is a gapless phase, where the system exhibits long-range spin-nematic order induced by parity-time symmetry breaking. Furthermore, we reveal that the parity-time symmetry breaking leads to the emergence of dynamical spin-nematic order, which also suggests a way of characterizing the spin-nematic phase diagram through non-equilibrium dynamics. Our findings show rich quantum phases stem from the competition among the Ising interaction, transverse field and non-Hermitian Gamma interaction, as well as providing a scheme for generating spin-nematic order in the spin chain.

</details>


### [112] [Propagation, generation, and utilization of topologically trivial magnetic solitons in magnetic nanowires](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17992)
*Kai-Tao Huang,X. S. Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究通过理论和数值方法研究了一维铁磁纳米线中拓扑平凡磁孤子的性质与生成，验证了近似解析解，发现了孤子在异质界面上的非线性反射/折射现象，提出了交替磁场或自旋极化电流脉冲生成孤子对的方法，并展示了孤子驱动畴壁运动的数字操控潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁孤子是磁性系统中的非线性局域激发，具有重要的物理特性和应用潜力。研究拓扑平凡磁孤子在铁磁纳米线中的性质与生成机制，旨在探索其在自旋电子学中的应用可能性，特别是利用孤子实现畴壁的数字操控，这与信息技术中的数字特性相兼容。

Method: 采用理论分析和数值模拟相结合的方法：1）理论推导一维拓扑平凡磁孤子的近似解析解，包含两个自由参数；2）通过微磁模拟验证解析解的正确性；3）研究孤子在具有不同各向异性的两种介质界面上的反射和折射行为；4）提出通过交替施加磁场或自旋极化电流脉冲在至少两个连续区域生成反向传播孤子对的方法；5）分析孤子驱动畴壁运动的机制。

Result: 1）验证了包含两个自由参数的近似解析孤子解与微磁模拟结果一致；2）发现孤子在异质界面上的反射和折射具有高度非线性特性，与线性自旋波明显不同；3）成功实现了通过交替磁场或电流脉冲生成反向传播孤子对，且每个孤子都符合可控的孤子解；4）证明磁孤子可用于驱动畴壁运动，运动距离由孤子幅度决定，实现了与数字信息技术兼容的离散畴壁操控。

Conclusion: 该研究系统探索了一维铁磁纳米线中拓扑平凡磁孤子的基本性质、生成方法和应用潜力。通过理论和数值验证了孤子解，发现了其独特的非线性界面行为，提出了实用的孤子生成方案，并展示了孤子在驱动畴壁数字操控方面的应用前景。这些发现为拓扑平凡孤子在自旋电子学领域的应用开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Magnetic solitons are nonlinear, local excitations in magnetic systems. In this study, we theoretically and numerically investigate the properties and generation of one-dimensional (1D) topologically trivial magnetic solitons in ferromagnetic nanowires. An approximate analytical soliton solution described by two free parameters is validated by comparing with the micromagnetic simulation. Across an interface between two media of different anisotropy, the reflection and refraction of a soliton are highly nonlinear that are different from the linear spin waves. A pair of magnetic solitons that propagate in opposite directions can be generated by alternately applying magnetic field or spin-polarized current pulses of opposite directions to at least two successive regions. Each soliton falls into a soliton solution that can be controlled by the generation process. These magnetic solitons can be used to drive domain wall motion over a certain distance determined by the soliton magnitude, allowing for discrete manipulation of domain walls compatible with the digital nature of information technology. Our findings pave the way for the application of topologically trivial solitons in spintronics.

</details>


### [113] [Generic skyrmion phase diagram in ferrimagnetic films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18079)
*M. V. Wijethunga,X. R. Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了亚晶格间交换耦合强度J如何影响铁磁斯格明子的稳定性，提出了参数ζ_eff来描述强/弱耦合交叉，揭示了在强耦合下可实现无DMI亚晶格的斯格明子稳定化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁磁斯格明子因其反铁磁耦合亚晶格和降低的净磁化而具有增强的可调性。现有研究通常使用参数κ来描述斯格明子稳定性，但该参数在铁磁系统中的适用性尚不明确，因为大多数研究假设固定的强亚晶格间交换耦合J。需要研究J变化如何影响铁磁斯格明子的稳定和亚稳态构型。

Method: 研究亚晶格间交换耦合J的变化对铁磁斯格明子弛豫稳定和亚稳态构型的影响。引入无量纲参数ζ_eff来表征强/弱亚晶格锁定之间的交叉。分析强耦合和弱耦合两种极限情况下的系统行为。

Result: 在强耦合区域，亚晶格间锁定使得在一个本征Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用(DMI)为零的亚晶格中也能稳定斯格明子，只要另一个亚晶格具有有限DMI，从而产生无DMI的铁磁斯格明子。随着J减小，这种锁定被破坏，导致亚晶格独立行为，基于有效κ的描述失效。建立了连接亚晶格间交换和斯格明子相稳定性的统一框架。

Conclusion: 研究建立了铁磁系统中亚晶格间交换耦合与斯格明子相稳定性之间的统一框架，揭示了J参数在决定铁磁斯格明子行为中的关键作用，为铁磁斯格明子的可调性和设计提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Ferrimagnetic skyrmions offer enhanced tunability due to antiferromagnetically coupled sublattices and reduced net magnetization. In chiral magnetic films at zero magnetic field, skyrmion stability is commonly characterized by a dimensionless parameter $κ$, yet its applicability to ferrimagnetic systems remains unclear, as most studies assume a fixed, strong inter-sublattice exchange coupling $J$. Here we investigate how variations in $J$ govern relaxed stable and metastable ferrimagnetic skyrmion configurations and introduce a dimensionless parameter $ζ_{eff}$ to characterize the crossover between strong and weak inter-sublattice locking. In the strong-coupling regime, inter-sublattice locking enables stabilization of skyrmion in a sublattice where intrinsic Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction is absent while the other sublattice has finite DMI, yielding a sublattice with DMI-free ferrimagnetic skyrmions. As $J$ decreases, this locking breaks down, leading to independent sublattice behavior and the failure of an effective $κ$-based description. Our results establish a unified framework linking inter-sublattice exchange and skyrmion phase stability in ferrimagnetic systems.

</details>


### [114] [Asymmetric Scattering-Induced Neel Spin-Orbit Torque in Antiferromagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18097)
*Sayan Sarkar,Amit Agarwal*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了反铁磁体中反常偏斜散射对奈尔自旋轨道扭矩的新贡献机制，该机制源于非对称杂质散射与布洛赫电子反常自旋极化率的耦合，可在足够杂质密度下超过传统对称散射贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上认为反铁磁体中的奈尔自旋轨道扭矩主要源于对称散射过程，但本研究旨在探索非对称杂质散射如何通过与布洛赫电子的反常自旋极化率耦合，产生额外的PT-奇偶性自旋极化，从而为电控反铁磁体开辟新途径。

Method: 采用四方晶系CuMnAs的最小模型，理论分析非对称杂质散射与反常自旋极化率的耦合机制，通过计算高阶散射过程与能带几何的相互作用，量化反常偏斜散射对奈尔自旋轨道扭矩的贡献。

Result: 反常偏斜散射贡献可与传统对称散射（德鲁德）贡献相当，在足够杂质密度下甚至可超过传统贡献。该机制将原本PT-偶极的磁化率转化为交变自旋极化，为奈尔自旋轨道扭矩提供了新的能带几何驱动机制。

Conclusion: 研究发现了基于能带几何的奈尔自旋轨道扭矩新机制，建立了电控反铁磁体的高效途径，表明非对称杂质散射在反铁磁开关中具有重要作用，为自旋电子学器件设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Magnetic switching in antiferromagnets relies on Neel spin orbit torque (NSOT), which originates from a current-induced staggered spin polarization of itinerant electrons. In collinear antiferromagnets, such a response requires the spin susceptibility to be odd under combined space-time inversion symmetry (PT), and is conventionally attributed to symmetric scattering processes. Here, we demonstrate that asymmetric impurity scattering generates an additional PT-odd spin polarization when coupled with the anomalous spin polarizability (ASP) of Bloch electrons. This extrinsic contribution arises from the interplay between antisymmetric higher-order scattering processes and band geometry, effectively converting an otherwise PT-even susceptibility into a staggered spin polarization. Using a minimal model of tetragonal CuMnAs, we show that this anomalous skew-scattering contribution can be comparable to, and with sufficient impurity density even exceed, the conventional symmetric scattering (Drude) contribution. Our results identify a new band-geometry-driven mechanism for NSOT and establish an efficient route for electrical control of antiferromagnets.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

# math-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [115] [Metric-Deformed Heisenberg Algebras and the $q$-Dirac Operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16508)
*Julio César Jaramillo Quiceno*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文引入了一族度量变形的海森堡代数M₁和M₂，其交换关系直接由对角洛伦兹度量的分量表示，统一了多个已知的q-变形海森堡代数，并建立了度量符号与变形参数之间的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立时空几何与q-变形量子代数之间的统一框架，通过度量变形的海森堡代数将不同的q-变形代数体系联系起来，为量子物理与几何结构提供更深刻的联系。

Method: 引入度量变形的海森堡代数M₁和M₂，其交换关系直接由对角洛伦兹度量的分量定义。利用西尔维斯特惯性定理建立度量符号与变形参数的关系。从变形的达朗贝尔算子构造q-狄拉克算子D_q，并证明D_q²恢复变形的克莱因-戈登算子。

Result: 证明了M₁和M₂代数统一了多个已知的q-变形海森堡代数，包括q-ħ代数、新q-海森堡代数和q-广义海森堡代数。建立了度量符号与变形参数之间的明确联系，并成功构造了q-狄拉克算子，其平方恢复变形的克莱因-戈登算子。

Conclusion: 通过度量变形的海森堡代数，本文建立了一个统一框架，将时空几何与q-变形量子代数联系起来，为理解量子物理中的变形代数结构提供了新的几何视角，并展示了如何从几何构造出发恢复量子场论中的基本算子。

Abstract: We introduce a family of metric-deformed Heisenberg algebras $M_1$ and $M_2$, where the commutation relations are expressed directly in terms of the components of a diagonal Lorentzian metric. We show that these algebras unify several known $q$-deformed Heisenberg algebras, including the $q$-$\hbar$ algebra, the new $q$-Heisenberg algebra, and the $q$-generalized Heisenberg algebra, which embed as special cases. Using Sylvester's theorem of inertia, we establish a connection between the metric signature and the deformation parameters. We construct a $q$-Dirac operator $D_q$ from the deformed D'Alembertian and prove that $D_q^2$ recovers the deformed Klein-Gordon operator. Furthermore, we relate this construction to the quadratic $q$-Dirac operator previously introduced by the author, providing a unified framework that bridges spacetime geometry and $q$-deformed quantum algebras.

</details>


### [116] [Quantum Reference Frames and Correlation Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16631)
*Claudio F. Paganini*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文为关联几何提供自包含、紧凑且易懂的入门介绍，该几何是因果费米子系统理论的基础，重点讨论规范变换处理方式，并论证关联几何描述物理系统在概念上更接近热力学而非量子理论


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为因果费米子系统理论的基础——关联几何提供清晰易懂的入门介绍，特别关注该框架如何处理规范变换（包括通过幺正等价原理处理微分同胚），并论证关联几何描述物理系统在概念上更接近热力学而非量子理论

Method: 采用自包含、紧凑的数学框架介绍关联几何的基本思想，重点阐述通过幺正等价原理处理规范变换和微分同胚的方法，将物理系统的基本描述建立在关联几何基础上

Result: 建立了关联几何的清晰理论框架，展示了该框架如何系统处理规范变换，论证了关联几何描述物理系统在概念上更接近热力学而非量子理论，为理解因果费米子系统提供了理论基础

Conclusion: 关联几何为物理系统提供了基于相关性的基本描述框架，该框架通过幺正等价原理有效处理规范变换，在概念上更接近热力学描述而非传统量子理论，为因果费米子系统理论奠定了坚实的几何基础

Abstract: The aim of this paper is to provide a largely self-contained, compact and comprehensible introduction to the basic ideas behind correlation geometry, which underlies the theory of causal fermion system (CFS). A key focus here is on the manner in which the framework deals with gauge transformations, including diffeomorphisms via the principle of unitary equivalence. We will argue that, conceptually, the fundamental description of a physical system in terms of its correlation geometry is much closer to thermodynamics than quantum theory.

</details>


### [117] [Continuum honeycomb Schrödinger operators with incommensurate line defects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16712)
*Pierre Amenoagbadji,Michael I. Weinstein*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 研究二维蜂窝结构中具有非公度（无理数）线缺陷或边缘的波传播，构造准周期边缘态，其能量在体谱隙中稠密分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非公度边缘的波传播问题，这类边缘缺乏沿边缘的平移不变性，使得边缘态的严格定义和构造变得困难，需要新的数学方法。

Method: 将二维哈密顿量表示为三维（退化椭圆）哈密顿量的限制，利用多尺度分析在三维设置中构造近似边缘态，通过限制得到沿无理边缘准周期的二维边缘态。

Result: 获得了三维哈密顿量的解析展开，其主导项是块对角狄拉克算子的解析；构造了无限多个边缘态本征对，其能量在扰动后的体谱隙中稠密分布。

Conclusion: 非公度边缘支持准周期边缘态，其能量在谱隙中稠密填充；全向非共振条件是构造的关键，该条件在强束缚区得到满足且与边缘无关。

Abstract: We study wave propagation in 2D honeycomb structures with a non-commensurate or ``irrational'' line defect or edge. Our model is a Schrödinger operator which interpolates, across the edge, between two distinct bulk (asymptotic) Hamiltonians with a common spectral gap about the ``Dirac point'' of an unperturbed honeycomb operator. We seek edge states, eigenstates that are bounded and oscillatory parallel to the edge, and decaying in the transverse direction. For non-commensurate edges, the rigorous definition of these states is nontrivial due to the lack of translation invariance along the edge. To address this, we exploit quasiperiodicity along the edge by expressing the Hamiltonian as the restriction of a 3D (degenerate elliptic) Hamiltonian describing a 3D medium with a 2D interface within which there is periodicity. Via multiscale analysis, we construct approximate edge states in this 3D setting and obtain by restriction 2D edge states which are quasiperiodic along the irrational edge. These edge states are seeded by eigenfunctions of an effective Dirac operator, which has an infinite block-diagonal structure due to the non-commensurate geometry. A consequence is that infinitely many edge state eigenpairs arise, whose energies are dense in the perturbed bulk spectral gap. In a forthcoming paper, we rigorously construct these gap-filling edge states under a Diophantine condition. The main result here is a key tool in this construction: a resolvent expansion for the 3D Hamiltonian, whose leading term is the resolvent of the block-diagonal Dirac operator. The validity of this expansion requires an omnidirectional non-resonance (no-fold) condition on the dispersion functions of the unperturbed honeycomb Hamiltonian. This condition is satisfied in the strong binding regime. In contrast with earlier works on commensurate edges, the omnidirectional condition is independent of the edge.

</details>


### [118] [On the asymptotic duality of spectral variances in random matrix theory and the "1/6" formula](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17150)
*Peng Tian,Roman Riser,Eugene Kanzieper*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文重新审视并证明了French等人提出的数方差与第L个有序特征值方差之间的"神秘"关系，对于β=2 Dyson对称类是渐近精确的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新研究French等人1978年提出的数方差与第L个有序特征值方差之间的"神秘"关系，旨在证明这种关系对于特定随机矩阵对称类的渐近精确性。

Method: 通过推导先前未知的能级间距自协方差求和规则来证明该关系，该推导基于作者先前关于随机矩阵理论中特征值涨落功率谱描述的工作。对β=2对称类进行解析证明，并对β=1和β=4对称类提出推测性扩展。

Result: 证明了French等人提出的关系对于β=2 Dyson对称类是渐近精确的，并提出了该关系在β=1和β=4对称类的推测性扩展。通过全面的数值分析验证了这些发现。

Conclusion: 该研究解决了随机矩阵理论中长期存在的数方差与有序特征值方差之间的关系问题，为β=2对称类提供了严格的数学证明，并为其他对称类的研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: A "mysterious" relation between the number variance and the variance of the $L$-th ordered eigenvalue, first suggested by French et al. [Ann. Phys. 113, 277 (1978)], is revisited and proven to be asymptotically exact for the $β=2$ Dyson symmetry class. Central to the proof is a previously unknown sum rule for the level spacing auto-covariances. Its derivation hinges on our previous work on the power spectrum description of eigenvalue fluctuations in random matrix theory. Analytical results for $β=2$ are complemented by conjectural extensions to the $β=1$ and $β=4$ symmetry classes. Our findings are corroborated by a comprehensive numerical analysis.

</details>


### [119] [Soliton-like solutions of the Camassa--Holm equation with variable coefficients and a small dispersion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17348)
*Yuliia Samoilenko,Valerii Samoilenko*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究具有可变系数的Camassa-Holm方程(vcCH方程)，构建具有小色散参数的类孤子和类峰子解，包括单相和双相情况，通过渐近展开方法获得任意精度的近似解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Camassa-Holm方程是描述浅水波传播的重要数学模型，而具有可变系数的vcCH方程是其直接推广。研究vcCH方程中具有小色散参数的类孤子和类峰子解，有助于理解这类非线性方程在更一般情况下的解结构特性，扩展经典孤子和峰子解的理论框架。

Method: 采用渐近展开方法，将解表示为正则背景项和奇异分量的和，其中奇异分量捕捉类孤子和类峰子解的特征。在单相情况下，确定主要奇异项，并在适当的函数空间中建立高阶奇异修正项的可解性。研究还涉及双相类孤子和类峰子解的构造。

Result: 证明了构造的渐近解的渐近精度定理。为每个考虑的情况提供了非平凡示例，根据获得的一般结果推导出显式形式的近似解，并展示了它们的图形表示。建立了在单相情况下高阶奇异修正项的可解性，使得能够以小参数构造任意精度的渐近解。

Conclusion: 成功构建了vcCH方程中具有小色散参数的类孤子和类峰子解的渐近展开，包括单相和双相情况。证明了渐近解的精度定理，并通过具体示例验证了理论结果。该方法为研究更一般的非线性波动方程提供了有效的数学工具。

Abstract: The paper deals with the Camassa--Holm equation with variable coefficients (vcCH equation) that is a direct generalization of the well known Camassa--Holm equation. We focus on the mathematical description of particular solutions of the vcCH equation with a small dispersion that exhibit properties analogous to those of classical soliton and peakon solutions, and consider the construction of soliton- and peakon-like solutions in the form of asymptotic expansions, including both one-phase and two-phase cases.
  The solution is expressed as the sum of a regular background common to all soliton- and peakon-like solutions and a singular component that captures their distinctive features, with the precise definition of the main singular term playing a central role. In the one-phase case, this term is determined, and the solvability of higher-order singular corrections is established in suitable functional spaces, enabling the construction of asymptotic solutions to arbitrary accuracy in a small parameter. The study also addresses the construction of two-phase soliton- and peakon-like solutions.
  Theorems on the asymptotic accuracy of the constructed asymptotic solutions have been proved. Each of the considered cases is illustrated by nontrivial examples for which, in accordance with the obtained general results, approximate solutions are derived in explicit form and their graphs are presented.

</details>


### [120] [Semiclassical resonances under local magnetic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17854)
*Pavel Exner,Ayman Kachmar*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 研究全平面紧支撑磁场下半经典磁拉普拉斯算子的共振，在局部恒定磁场假设下，证明了Landau能级附近存在虚部指数小的半经典共振


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究半经典磁拉普拉斯算子在磁场存在时的共振现象，特别关注磁场局部恒定、磁阶跃不连续、磁阱以及磁场零点等特殊几何结构对共振产生的影响

Method: 采用半经典复标度法和黑盒散射理论框架，分析紧支撑磁场的全平面系统，在局部恒定磁场假设下进行理论推导

Result: 证明了在Landau能级附近存在虚部指数小的半经典共振；共振可从弯曲界面上的磁阶跃不连续、非退化磁阱中产生；若磁场有孤立零点，则在非简谐Landau能级附近也会出现共振

Conclusion: 磁场几何结构（局部恒定、阶跃不连续、磁阱、零点）对半经典磁拉普拉斯算子的共振产生有重要影响，这些结果为理解量子系统中磁场效应提供了理论依据

Abstract: We study resonances for the semiclassical magnetic Laplacian in the full plane with a compactly supported magnetic field in the framework of semiclassical complex scaling and black box scattering theory. Assuming that the magnetic field is locally constant, we prove the existence of semiclassical resonances near the Landau levels with exponentially small imaginary parts. We also prove that resonances emerge from a magnetic step discontinuity along a curved interface or a non-degenerate magnetic well, and in the vicinity of anharmonic Landau levels if the field has an isolated zero.

</details>


### [121] [Synthetic Seismograms from Particle Bed Interactions and Turbulent River Flow: Modeling and Comparison with Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17913)
*Sara Nicoletti,Giacomo Belli,Omar Morandi,Emanuele Marchetti*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于物理的数值模型，通过模拟砾石河床中单个颗粒轨迹、碰撞滚动力、湍流和涡旋脱落，预测水流-沉积物运动产生的地震辐射，用于区分沉积物输运与水流对河流地震噪声的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法难以区分河流地震噪声中沉积物输运和水流湍流的贡献，需要建立能够从颗粒尺度动力学出发、物理机制明确的数值模型来解析这些不同源的地震信号特征。

Method: 开发基于物理的数值模型：1) 模拟单个颗粒轨迹；2) 从颗粒尺度动力学评估碰撞和滚动力；3) 考虑水柱中的宽带湍流和涡旋脱落；4) 使用瑞利波格林函数方法将合成地震信号传播到接收器；5) 估计合成地面速度信号。

Result: 1) 控制测试案例显示间歇性、粒径选择性沉积物输运机制产生独特的光谱特征；2) 与托斯卡纳亚平宁山脉山洪事件地震数据对比，观测频率带与模型预测基本一致；3) 阐明了颗粒碰撞与湍流相对宽度；4) 验证了颗粒尺度动力学解析可区分沉积物输运和水流诱导的地震噪声贡献。

Conclusion: 基于物理的颗粒尺度动力学数值模型为区分河流地震噪声中沉积物输运和水流贡献提供了有效框架，能够解析不同输运机制的光谱特征，有助于更准确地监测和解释河流沉积过程。

Abstract: We present a physics based numerical model that estimates the seismic radiation generated by water sediment flows in gravel-bed rivers. The model reproduces the trajectories of individual particles, evaluates impact and rolling forces from grain scale dynamics, and accounts for broadband turbulence and vortex shedding in the water column. Synthetic seismic signals are propagated to the receivers using the Rayleigh wave Green s function approach and synthetic ground-velocity signals are estimated. Application to a controlled test case shows how intermittent, size selective sediment transport mechanisms produce distinct spectral signatures. Comparison with seismic data from a flood event in a mountain torrent in the Tuscan Apennines displays general agreement with the observed frequency bands and clarifies the relative width of particle collisions and turbulent flow. These results show that resolved grain scale dynamics provides a framework for discriminating sediment transport and flow induced contributions to river seismic noise.

</details>


### [122] [Spherical singularities in compactified Ruijsenaars--Schneider systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18023)
*L. Feher,H. R. Dullin*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究了通过SU(n)拟哈密顿对偶约化构造的李维尔可积系统，这些系统可视为紧致化的三角Ruijsenaars-Schneider系统，并分析了类型(ii)情况下奇异纤维的几何结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究通过SU(n)拟哈密顿对偶约化构造的李维尔可积系统的奇异纤维结构，特别是类型(ii)情况下作用映射奇异纤维的几何性质，以丰富具有球面奇点的李维尔可积系统示例。

Method: 通过SU(n)拟哈密顿对偶约化构造李维尔可积系统，分析参数y取值对系统类型的影响，研究类型(ii)情况下奇异纤维的几何结构，建立纤维模型作为SU(n)子群相对于另一子群作用的商空间。

Result: 证明了所有奇异纤维都是光滑连通各向同性子流形；建立了纤维的群论模型；确定了n≥4且π/(n-1)<y<π/(n-2)时作用变量填充多面体的顶点，并证明奇异顶点上的纤维微分同胚于S³≃SU(2)。

Conclusion: 该研究丰富了具有球面奇点的李维尔可积系统示例，深入理解了通过SU(n)拟哈密顿对偶约化构造的紧致化三角Ruijsenaars-Schneider系统的奇异纤维几何结构。

Abstract: We investigate certain Liouville integrable systems constructed earlier via reduction of the quasi-Hamiltonian double of $\mathrm{SU}(n)$. These systems live on compact connected symplectic manifolds of dimension $2(n-1)$ and can be interpreted as compactified trigonometric Ruijsenaars--Schneider systems. Depending on the value of a parameter $0<y< π$, they arise in two drastically different forms: in type (i) these are toric systems, while in the type (ii) cases they possess globally continuous action variables that generate a Hamiltonian torus action (only) on a dense open subset of the phase space. The principal goal of the paper is to study those fibers of the action map (alias the $\mathbb{T}^{n-1}$ momentum map) which are contained in the complement of the domain of the densely defined torus action occurring in the type (ii) cases. We demonstrate that all such `singular fibers' are smooth connected isotropic submanifolds. We also work out a model of the fibers as quotient spaces of certain subgroups of $\mathrm{SU}(n)$ with respect to an action of another subgroup. The general results are exemplified by determining the vertices of the polytope filled by the action variables in the simplest type (ii) cases that appear for any $n\geq 4$ with $π/(n-1) <y < π/(n-2)$, and proving that the fibers over the `singular vertices' are diffeomorphic to $S^3 \simeq \mathrm{SU}(2)$ in these cases. In this way, our findings enrich the set of examples of Liouville integrable systems with spherical singularities.

</details>


### [123] [Moments at the hard edge and Rayleigh functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18113)
*Anna Maltsev,Nick Simm*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 研究拉盖尔随机矩阵系综的逆幂迹矩，在硬边极限下分析其渐近行为，特别关注经典β值和一般β>0情况，并在低温极限下建立与贝塞尔zeta函数的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受随机矩阵谱矩与关联zeta函数之间类比的启发，研究拉盖尔系综的逆幂迹矩，探索其在硬边极限下的渐近行为，旨在建立随机矩阵理论与zeta函数理论之间的深层联系。

Method: 采用硬边极限（大N极限下关注低特征值区域）分析拉盖尔系综的逆幂迹矩。对于经典情况β∈{1,2,4}，获得显式结果并扩展到相应的Mellin变换。对于一般β>0，利用Fyodorov和Le Doussal的结果，通过分区求和公式表示矩。最后考虑低温极限β→∞（同时N→∞），分析矩的渐近行为。

Result: 在经典β值情况下获得了逆矩的显式表达式和相应的Mellin变换公式。对于一般β>0，得到了基于分区求和的矩表达式。在低温极限β→∞（N→∞）下，证明了矩由贝塞尔zeta函数给出，建立了随机矩阵矩与特殊函数之间的精确对应关系。

Conclusion: 拉盖尔系综的逆幂迹矩在硬边极限下展现出丰富的数学结构，经典情况有显式解，一般情况可用分区和表示，低温极限下与贝塞尔zeta函数精确对应，这深化了随机矩阵谱统计与zeta函数理论之间的联系。

Abstract: Motivated by the analogy between spectral moments of random matrices and associated zeta functions, we study inverse power trace moments of the Laguerre ensemble of dimension $N$ and inverse temperature parameter $β>0$. We consider a large $N$ regime determined by the low-lying eigenvalues of the ensemble known as the hard edge. In the classical cases $β\in \{1,2,4\}$, we obtain explicit results for the inverse moments and extend these to formulae for the corresponding Mellin transforms. In the case of general $β>0$, by a result of Fyodorov and Le Doussal, we obtain a different formula for the moments given as a sum over partitions. We use this to consider a low temperature limit where $β\to \infty$ as $N \to \infty$. In this limit, we show that the moments are given in terms of the Bessel zeta function.

</details>


### [124] [Sketch of a Gauge Model of Gravity with SU(2) Symmetry in Minkowski space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18280)
*Nikolay Marchuk*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 提出一个具有SU(2)对称性的规范模型，在闵可夫斯基空间中描述基本费米子（轻子和夸克）的引力相互作用，使用新型狄拉克方程替代标准狄拉克方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准模型使用U(2)规范对称性描述电弱相互作用，SU(3)描述QCD相互作用，但缺乏对引力相互作用的规范理论描述。该模型旨在为基本费米子的引力相互作用提供一个基于规范理论的框架。

Method: 采用2002年发明的新型狄拉克型方程替代标准狄拉克方程，该方程具有额外的SU(2)规范对称性。将对应于该SU(2)对称性的杨-米尔斯场识别为相互作用基本费米子的引力场，并运用克利福德分析的元素。

Result: 建立了一个具有SU(2)规范对称性的引力模型，将引力场解释为杨-米尔斯场，为基本费米子的引力相互作用提供了规范理论框架。

Conclusion: 该模型成功地将引力相互作用纳入规范理论框架，通过SU(2)规范对称性和新型狄拉克方程，为基本费米子的引力描述提供了新的理论途径。

Abstract: We propose a gauge model with the SU(2) symmetry, which describes a gravitational interaction of fundamental fermions (leptons and quarks) in the Minkowski space. In the Standard Model one uses a Dirac-Yang-Mills system of equations with U(2) gauge symmetry for electroweak interactions and with SU(3) gauge symmetry for QCD interactions. A key idea of the model is to use the Dirac-type equation (invented in 2002) instead of the standard Dirac equation. This Dirac-type equation has an additional SU(2) gauge symmetry. The Yang-Mills field, which corresponds to this SU(2) symmetry, we identify with the gravitational field of interacted fundamental fermions. Some elements of Clifford analysis are used in the model.

</details>


### [125] [Bounding relative entropy for non-unitary excitations in quantum field theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18383)
*Markus B. Fröb,Leonardo Sangaletti*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 利用非交换L^p范数的凸性来界定冯·诺依曼代数上忠实态与任意激发态之间的相对熵


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对量子场论中普遍存在的III型冯·诺依曼代数（如局部代数），提供一种不依赖于相对模算子的相对熵界定方法

Method: 利用非交换L^p范数的凸性性质，建立忠实态与任意激发态之间相对熵的数学界定框架

Result: 证明了对于光线上手征流，真空态与稠密单粒子态集合之间的相对熵具有一致有界性

Conclusion: 该方法适用于一般冯·诺依曼代数，为量子场论中相对熵的研究提供了新的数学工具

Abstract: We show how one can use the convexity of non-commutative $L^p$ norms to bound the relative entropy between a faithful state on a von Neumann algebra and an arbitrary excitation thereof. Our results hold for general von Neumann algebras, including the local algebras of type III that are ubiquitous in quantum field theory, and do not require knowledge of the relative modular operator. As an application of our results, we prove that for the chiral current on a light ray, the relative entropy between the vacuum and a dense set of single-particle states is uniformly bounded.

</details>


### [126] [Eigenvalue asymptotics of Müller minimizers for atoms and molecules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18386)
*Rupert L. Frank,Long Meng,Phan Thành Nam,Heinz Siedentop*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 研究Müller泛函极小化子的谱性质，证明在特定条件下第k个特征值以A_*k^{-8/3}渐近衰减


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究原子和分子中Müller泛函极小化子的谱性质，特别是特征值的渐近行为，这对于理解量子多体系统的电子结构理论具有重要意义

Method: 基于Sobolev关于薛定谔基态单粒子密度矩阵渐近行为的工作，但需要发展新方法处理Müller理论中极小化子积分核在近对角线的奇异行为和无穷远处的衰减性质

Result: 在Z和N满足适当假设条件下，证明Müller极小化子γ_*的第k个特征值当k→∞时表现为A_*k^{-8/3}，其中常数A_*>0由γ_*的密度显式确定；在原子情况下，当Z足够大且N≤Z-C_0Z^{1/3}时假设成立

Conclusion: 成功建立了Müller泛函极小化子特征值的精确渐近行为，为量子多体系统的电子结构理论提供了重要的数学基础，扩展了Sobolev的工作并发展了处理奇异核和衰减性质的新技术

Abstract: We study the spectral properties of minimizers of the Müller functional for atoms and molecules with $N$ electrons and total nuclear charge $Z$. We prove that under some suitable assumptions on $Z$ and $N$, the $k$-th eigenvalue of a Müller minimizer $γ_*$ behaves as $A_* k^{-8/3}$ when $k\to \infty$, with a constant $A_*>0$ determined explicitly by the density of $γ_*$. In particular, in the atomic case $V=Z|x|^{-1}$ our assumption holds if $Z$ is sufficiently large and $N\le Z- C_0 Z^{1/3}$. While our proof is inspired by Sobolev's work on the asymptotic behavior of the one-particle density matrix of Schrödinger ground states, the analysis in Müller theory requires several new ingredients concerning both the singular behavior of the integral kernel of the minimizers near the diagonal and the decay properties at infinity.

</details>


### [127] [Kernel-Preserving Dynamics and Symmetry Classification for Synchronization Subspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18448)
*Nicholas R. Allgood*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 研究张量积希尔伯特空间中同步子空间的保持与稳定性，建立ε-相容动力学的漂移界，并在群对称下刻画同步保持动力学的代数结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有限维希尔伯特空间张量积中同步子空间的动力学行为，旨在理解局部子系统自伴算子定义的同步子空间在量子动力学中的保持条件和稳定性。

Method: 定义同步子空间为差算子K的核，研究ε-相容动力学（满足[H,K]范数≤ε）的漂移行为，并在有限群对称性下利用张量积分解和等变分量分析同步子空间结构。

Result: 1. 对于ε-相容动力学，证明初始同步状态的偏离速率至多为线性时间乘以ε，且通过显式构造证明该估计在主导阶是最优的；2. 在有限群对称下，同步子空间与张量积分解中的对角等变分量重合，并刻画了同步保持动力学的代数为群作用和同步算子交换子的交集。

Conclusion: 建立了张量积系统中同步子空间稳定性的精确数学框架，提供了ε-相容动力学的尖锐漂移界，并在群对称下完全描述了保持同步的动力学的代数结构，为量子同步和相干控制提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We study the preservation and stability of synchronization subspaces in tensor products of finite-dimensional Hilbert spaces. Given self-adjoint operators $T_A$ and $T_B$ on local subsystems, the synchronization subspace is defined as the kernel of the difference operator $K = T_A \otimes I - I \otimes T_B$. We establish two main results: First for $ε$-compatible dynamics satisfying $||[H,K]|| \leq ε$, we prove a sharp drift bound where any initially synchronized state deviates from the kernel at a rate at most linear in time with slope $ε$. We show by explicit construction that this estimate is optimal to leading order. Second in the presence of finite group symmetry, we show that the synchronization subspace coincides with the diagonal isotypic component in the tensor product decomposition and we characterize the algebra of synchronization-preserving dynamics as the intersection of the commutants of the group action and synchronization operator.

</details>


### [128] [Low-noise Pauli-consistent ensemble Monte Carlo for graphene with electron-electron scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18517)
*Tigran Zalinyan,Giovanni Nastasi*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于石墨烯电子-电子散射模拟的采样伙伴近似方法，显著降低计算成本，使大规模低噪声模拟成为可能，同时识别并处理了数值振荡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在石墨烯的显式带内电子-电子散射模拟中，电子-电子提议率评估的计算成本很高，限制了大规模低噪声模拟的实现。

Method: 引入采样伙伴近似，用瞬时系综的均匀采样替代完整的伙伴单元求和，同时保持事件级碰撞步骤不变，显著降低计算成本。

Result: 与完整求和参考相比，采样伙伴近似显示出良好的一致性，同时计算成本大幅降低，使大规模低噪声模拟成为可能，并清晰识别出系统性的数值振荡。

Conclusion: 采样伙伴近似是有效的计算加速方法，识别出的数值振荡源于离散动量空间网格上的确定性漂移，并提出了在不修改底层蒙特卡洛动力学的情况下减少其对观测值影响的方法。

Abstract: We investigate Pauli-consistent ensemble Monte Carlo simulations of graphene with explicit intraband electron-electron scattering. To reduce the cost of electron-electron proposal-rate evaluation, we introduce a sampled-partner approximation that replaces the full partner-cell sum by uniform sampling from the instantaneous ensemble, while leaving the event-level collision step unchanged. Comparison with the full-sum reference shows close agreement together with a substantial reduction in computational cost, enabling large-ensemble low-noise simulations. In this regime, systematic oscillatory components become clearly resolved in ensemble-averaged time traces. We show that these oscillations are numerical and originate from deterministic drift on the discretized momentum-space grid. We also discuss a procedure for reducing their impact in recorded observables without modifying the underlying Monte Carlo dynamics.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.supr-con'></div>

# cond-mat.supr-con [[Back]](#toc)

### [129] [Signature of Unconventional Superconductivity in the High Temperature Normal State Resistivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16433)
*Yuchen Wu,Yiwen Liu,Wanyue Lin,Zohar Nussinov,Sheng Ran*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 机器学习分析发现铁基超导体中正常态电阻率与超导性存在强相关性，预测信息存在于远高于Tc的150-300K温区，超导特征分布于多个散射通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示非常规超导性与正常态之间的联系是理解配对机制的关键，以往研究主要关注Tc附近的温度范围，需要探索更宽温度范围内正常态与超导性的关联。

Method: 使用机器学习方法分析铁基超导体的正常态电阻率数据，识别与超导性相关的预测特征，特别关注150-300K温度窗口内的信息。

Result: 发现正常态电阻率与超导性存在强相关性，预测信息主要存在于150-300K的宽温度窗口（远高于Tc），且超导特征分布于多个散射通道中。

Conclusion: 铁基超导体中正常态与超导性的关联延伸至远高于Tc的温度范围，超导特征分布于多个散射通道，这为理解非常规超导机制提供了新视角，需要进一步理论研究。

Abstract: Unconventional superconductivity remains one of the central unsolved problems in quantum materials, and revealing its connection to the normal state is widely believed to be key to uncovering the pairing mechanism. Previous efforts have largely focused on the temperature range immediately above the superconducting transition, where specific scattering channels-such as strange-metal transport-have been identified as sharing a possible microscopic origin with superconductivity. Here, using machine learning, we demonstrate a strong correlation between normal-state resistivity and superconductivity in Fe-based superconductors. Remarkably, the predictive information reside in a wide window of 150-300 K, far above $T_c$ of this family. We further show that the signatures of superconductivity are distributed across multiple scattering channels, which requires further theoretical investigation.

</details>


### [130] [Quantum Tunnelling and Room-Temperature Superconductivity of Hydride from Size Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16460)
*Xiaozhi Hu*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该论文提出将极端压力下金属探针间微米级氢化物样品的超导性视为金属-氢化物-金属的宏观量子隧穿现象，通过调控压力调节能垒高度，减小能垒宽度和样品厚度以优化量子隧穿，实现室温超导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索实现室温超导的新途径，将极端压力下金属探针间氢化物样品的超导现象解释为宏观量子隧穿过程，为理解高压超导机制提供新视角。

Method: 将金属-氢化物-金属结构视为量子隧穿系统：1) 通过压力调控氢化物能垒高度；2) 减小金属探针尖端间距以最小化能垒宽度，降低电子隧穿指数衰减概率；3) 使用约1微米薄氢化物样品优化隧穿条件。

Result: 提出通过同时减小能垒宽度和样品厚度可以优化量子隧穿条件，为实现更高超导温度（包括室温超导）提供理论框架和实验指导。

Conclusion: 为实现室温超导，建议同时减小金属-氢化物-金属结构中的能垒宽度和氢化物样品厚度，以优化量子隧穿条件，这为高压氢化物超导研究提供了新的物理解释和实验方向。

Abstract: Superconductivity of a micron-sized hydride sample measured between metal probes under extreme pressure could be considered as a macroscopic quantum tunnelling phenomenon through metal-hydride-metal. The energy barrier height of hydride is regulated by pressure. The energy barrier width between tips of the metal probes should be minimized to limit the chance of exponential decay in electron tunnelling. There is also a thickness effect since thinner hydride samples around 1 micron are favoured for achieving higher superconductive temperatures. Hence, reduction in both barrier width and sample thickness is recommended to ensure optimum quantum tunnelling for realization of the room temperature superconductivity.

</details>


### [131] [Ultrafast Magneto-Pressure Spectroscopy and Control of Correlated Phases in a Trilayer Nickelate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16611)
*Zhi Xiang Chong,Joong-Mok Park,Shuyuan Huyan,Avinash Khatri,Martin Mootz,Xinglong Chen,Daniel P. Phelan,Liang Luo,Ilias E. Perakis,J. F. Mitchell,Sergey L. Bud'ko,Paul C. Canfield,Jigang Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 开发了高压（40 GPa）、强磁场（7 T）、低温（5 K）飞秒光谱平台，用于研究Pr₄Ni₃O₁₀中的准粒子动力学，观察到电荷密度波转变处的临界减慢现象，并发现高压下可能存在非体相的超导相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同时施加高压和强磁场的超快光谱技术对于研究压力驱动的电子不稳定性、关联相以及极端条件下的体超导行为至关重要，但此前缺乏这样的实验平台，阻碍了对非平衡超导性和涡旋控制准粒子动力学的深入理解。

Method: 开发了高压（最高40 GPa）、强磁场（最高7 T）、低温（最低5 K）飞秒光谱实验平台，并将其应用于研究三层镍酸盐Pr₄Ni₃O₁₀中的磁压演化准粒子动力学。

Result: 在电荷密度波转变处观察到显著的准粒子弛豫临界减慢现象，该现象在施加压力后消失。在更高压力下，低温弛豫时间反而延长，这与初生超导相关性一致。然而，高达7 T的磁场依赖性可忽略不计，且未观察到涡旋诱导的预瓶颈动力学——这些在对照体超导样品中观察到的稳健特征表明，在当前压力条件下任何超导态都可能是非体相、丝状或强不均匀的。

Conclusion: 磁压超快光谱能力为解决关联量子材料中压力诱导超导性和交织序的突出问题开辟了新途径，为研究极端条件下的非平衡超导现象提供了重要工具。

Abstract: Ultrafast spectroscopy under simultaneous high pressure and magnetic field provides a versatile approach for investigating pressure-driven electronic instabilities and correlated phases, and for probing potential bulk superconducting behavior under extreme conditions. However, such an experimental platform has yet to be implemented, standing as a roadblock to a fuller understanding of nonequilibrium superconductivity and vortex-controlled quasi-particle (QP) dynamics.
  Here, we bridge this capability gap by developing high pressure (up to 40 GPa), high magnetic field (up to 7 T), cryogenic (down to 5 K) femtosecond spectroscopy, and using it to probe magneto-pressure evolution of quasiparticle dynamics in the trilayer nickelate $\mathrm{Pr}_4\mathrm{Ni}_3\mathrm{O}_{10}$.
  We observe pronounced critical slowing down of QP relaxation at the charge-density-wave transition, which collapses under applied pressure. At higher pressures, the relaxation instead lengthens at low temperature, consistent with incipient superconducting correlations.
  However, the negligibel magnetic-field-dependence up to 7~T and absence of vortex-induced pre-bottleneck dynamics--robust signatures observed in our controlled bulk superconducting samples--indicates that any superconducting state under the present pressure conditions is likely non-bulk, filamentary, or strongly inhomogeneous.
  The magneto-pressure ultrafast capability opens a new avenue for resolving outstanding questions surrounding pressure-induced superconductivity and intertwined orders in correlated quantum materials.

</details>


### [132] [Pairing Mechanism in Bilayer Nickelate La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$ Superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17181)
*Xianxin Wu,Tao Xiang,Jiangping Hu*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 双层镍酸盐La₃Ni₂O₇中发现Tc≈80K超导性，论文通过基因原理和协作费米面规则，识别出两个反铁磁交换通道主导配对，产生具有内部符号反转的s±超导态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索双层镍酸盐La₃Ni₂O₇中Tc≈80K超导性的微观机制，验证先前提出的基因原理和协作费米面规则能否统一解释这一新型高温超导体系。

Method: 应用基因原理和协作费米面规则分析双层多轨道系统，识别两个主要反铁磁交换通道：层间轨道内最近邻交换J⊥(d_z²轨道)和层内轨道间最近邻交换J_xz(d_z²和d_x²-y²轨道)。

Result: 两个交换通道协同产生稳健的s±超导态，在动量空间中镜面对称偶数和奇数费米面口袋间存在内部符号反转。配对通道在β口袋上最大化超导能隙，具有(cosk_x-cosk_y)²动量空间形式因子。

Conclusion: La₃Ni₂O₇符合非常规超导的统一框架，同时揭示了高温配对的新颖电子环境，为理解高温超导的组织原理提供了新视角。

Abstract: The recent discovery of superconductivity with $T_c \approx 80$~K in bilayer nickelate La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$ provides a new setting in which to test the organizing principles of unconventional high-temperature superconductivity. We show that the gene principle and the collaborative Fermi-surface rule which were previously proposed to unify unconventional high temperature superconductors, extend naturally to this bilayer, multi-orbital system. We identify that there are two antiferromagnetic exchange channels that can provide the dominant pairing force: an interlayer intra-orbital nearest-neighbour exchange $J_\perp$ between $d_{z^2}$ orbitals mediated by the inner apical oxygen, and an intralayer inter-orbital nearest-neighbour exchange $J_{xz}$ between $d_{z^2}$ and $d_{x^2-y^2}$ orbitals mediated by the in-plane oxygen. Owing to the bilayer bonding--antibonding splitting and the $B_{1g}$ symmetry of the $d_{x^2-y^2}$ orbital, these two channels cooperate to produce a robust $s^\pm$ superconducting state with an internal sign reversal between mirror-even and mirror-odd Fermi-surface pockets in momentum space. Both pairing channels maximize the superconducting gap on the $β$ pocket with a form factor $(cosk_x-cosk_y)^2$ in momentum space. The result places La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$ within a unified framework for unconventional superconductivity while revealing a distinct electronic environment for high-$T_c$ pairing.

</details>


### [133] [Collective Resonance of Superconducting/Normal Domain Walls in the Intermediate State of type-I superconductor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17333)
*Mengju Yuan,Yugang Zhang,Ying Zhu,Jingchun Gao,Aifeng Wang,Mingquan He,Jun-Yi Ge,Yisheng Chai*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 使用交流磁致伸缩技术揭示铅中间态中超导/正常界面集体振荡的准共振响应


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统磁测量方法受表面势垒和过阻尼磁通过程主导，难以观测I型超导体中相边界（如超导/正常界面）的动力学行为

Method: 采用交流磁致伸缩作为灵敏探针，测量铅中间态中S/N界面在交变磁场下的响应，分析其频率依赖特性

Result: 发现了与德拜型弛豫不同的准共振响应：虚部符号反转，实部随频率非单调变化；该行为源于正常畴内涡流驱动的S/N界面集体振荡

Conclusion: 揭示了超导调制相中的基本动力学通道，确立了交流磁致伸缩系数作为探测隐藏界面物理的有力工具

Abstract: The dynamics of phase boundaries, such as superconducting/normal (S/N) interfaces in type-I superconductors, are typically obscured in conventional magnetic measurements, which are dominated by surface barriers and over-damped flux processes. Here, we employ ac magnetostriction as a sensitive probe to reveal the distinct bulk dynamics of these domain walls in the intermediate state of lead. In contrast to the Debye-type relaxation observed in magnetic susceptibility, we discover a pronounced quasiresonant response characterized by a sign reversal of the imaginary component and a non-monotonic evolution of the real part with frequency. We attribute this behavior to the collective oscillations of S/N interfaces driven by eddy currents generated within the normal domains. This work uncovers a fundamental dynamical channel in superconducting modulated phases and establishes ac magnetostrictive coefficient as a powerful tool for probing hidden interface physics.

</details>


### [134] [Anisotropic Superconducting Diode Effect in Planar Josephson Junctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17594)
*Abhishek Chilampankunnel Prasannan,Baris Pekerten,Nowar Alashkar,Alex Matos-Abiague*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该理论研究在面内磁场下具有Rashba和Dresselhaus自旋轨道耦合的邻近平面约瑟夫森结中超导二极管效应的磁性和晶体各向异性，揭示了对称性约束、电场门控诱导的极性反转等关键特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究超导二极管效应(SDE)在具有自旋轨道耦合(SOC)的平面约瑟夫森结中的各向异性行为，理解SOC与塞曼相互作用之间的相互作用机制，为实验探测半导体基平面约瑟夫森结中的SDE提供理论指导。

Method: 采用对称性分析识别抑制SDE的几何约束条件；建立唯象模型描述二极管效率与自旋轨道场和磁场相对取向的关系；在窄结、低场区域使用解析方法将二极管响应各向异性与SOC诱导的费米面畸变和各向异性库珀对动量联系起来；通过Bogoliubov-de Gennes方程的紧束缚模拟验证理论预测。

Result: 对称性分析确定了磁场和晶体学取向的几何约束条件，在这些条件下SDE与场强无关地被抑制；唯象模型显示二极管效率取决于自旋轨道场和磁场的相对取向；模拟重现了实验趋势，揭示电场门控可在低场区域诱导SDE极性反转，即使只有Rashba SOC；预测了特定场取向、结几何和SOC比例下的额外反转。

Conclusion: 该研究阐明了各向异性非互易超导输运的起源，为实验探测半导体基平面约瑟夫森结中超导二极管效应的机制提供了指导，揭示了SOC与塞曼相互作用之间的复杂相互作用及其在SDE各向异性中的关键作用。

Abstract: We theoretically investigate the magnetic and crystalline anisotropies of the superconducting diode effect (SDE) in proximitized planar Josephson junctions (JJs) with coexisting Rashba and Dresselhaus spin-orbit couplings (SOCs) under an in-plane magnetic field. A symmetry analysis identifies geometric constraints on magnetic-field and crystallographic orientations for which the SDE is suppressed independently of field strength, providing experimentally testable signatures of the interplay between SOC and Zeeman interaction. We develop a phenomenological model showing that the diode efficiency depends on the relative alignment between spin-orbit and magnetic fields, and corroborate this behavior in the narrow-junction, low-field regime using an analytical approach that links the anisotropy of the diode response to SOC-induced Fermi surface distortions and anisotropic Cooper pair momentum. These findings are supported by tight-binding simulations of the Bogoliubov-de Gennes equation, which reproduce recent experimental trends. The simulations reveal that electrostatic gating can induce polarity reversals of the SDE in the low-field regime even with only Rashba SOC, consistent with recent experiments, and predict additional reversals for specific field orientations, junction geometries, and SOC ratios. Our results elucidate the origin of anisotropic nonreciprocal superconducting transport and provide guidance for experimentally probing the mechanisms underlying the SDE in semiconductor-based planar JJs.

</details>


### [135] [Metal Atom (Dis)Order and Superconductivity in YCaH$_{n}$ ($n=8-20$) High-Pressure Superhydrides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17712)
*Masashi W. Kimura,Seong Won Jang,Nisha Geng,Eva Zurek*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 通过DFT计算研究Y-Ca超氢化物的结构、性质及金属原子无序性，发现YCaH₈存在多个近等焓相，表明构型熵对稳定性重要；YCaH₈在180 GPa下Tc可达149-170 K；YCaH₁₂的Tc范围105-253 K；YCaH₁₈和YCaH₂₀仅预测到单一稳定结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高压超氢化物因其高Tc而备受关注，但二元超氢化物中金属原子无序性对结构和超导性能的影响尚不清楚。本研究旨在通过DFT计算探究Y-Ca超氢化物的结构、金属原子无序性以及掺杂对Tc的影响。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论（DFT）计算研究YCaHₙ（n=8-20）的结构和性质。分析金属原子排列不同的多个相，评估构型熵对稳定性的影响。使用各向同性Eliashberg形式计算超导临界温度Tc。

Result: YCaH₈存在多个金属原子排列不同但近等焓的相，表明构型熵对稳定性重要。在180 GPa下，P4/mmm和Cmmm YCaH₈的Tc分别为149 K和170 K。YCaH₁₂也存在无序性，有序变体的Tc范围宽达105-253 K（200 GPa）。YCaH₁₈和YCaH₂₀仅预测到单一动态稳定结构，这与稳定二元母体结构差异有关。

Conclusion: Y-Ca超氢化物中金属原子无序性显著影响结构稳定性和超导性能。YCaH₈的等摩尔金属原子比例使费米能级位于态密度峰值，显著增强Tc。YCaH₁₂的Tc对掺杂敏感，可轻微增强或大幅降低。结构差异导致YCaH₁₈和YCaH₂₀仅存在单一稳定相。这些发现为设计高性能超氢化物超导体提供了重要见解。

Abstract: High-pressure superhydrides have attracted much attention due to their high superconducting critical temperatures ($T_\text{c}$s). Herein, density functional theory (DFT) calculations are used to study the structures and properties, including potential for metal atom disorder and doping-enhanced $T_\text{c}$, within Y-Ca superhydrides with YCaH$_{n}$ ($n=8-20$) compositions. For YCaH$_8$ numerous phases that differed in the arrangement of the metal atoms were found to be nearly isoenthalpic, suggesting the importance of configurational entropy on stability. The equimolar ratio of the two metal atoms brought the Fermi level to a peak in the density of states, enhancing $T_\text{c}$ to 149~K and 170~K for $P4/mmm$ and $Cmmm$ YCaH$_{8}$, respectively, at 180~GPa within the isotropic Eliashberg formalism. YCaH$_{12}$ was also predicted to be disordered, however the $T_\text{c}$s of the ordered variants spanned a wide range from 105-253~K at 200~GPa, showing that doping could either mildly enhance or drastically reduce $T_\text{c}$ from that of the parent compounds. For YCaH$_{18}$ and YCaH$_{20}$, only a single dynamically stable superhydride was predicted, which we attribute to the differences in the structures of the stable binary parents.

</details>


### [136] [Pairing properties of correlated three-leg ladders with strong interchain couplings near 1/3 filling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17812)
*Yushi Yamada,Tatsuya Kaneko,Masataka Kakoi,Ryota Ueda,Kazuhiko Kuroki*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 研究强链间耦合三腿t-J梯子在1/3填充附近的基态性质，发现空穴掺杂产生幂律衰减的配对关联，而电子掺杂无显著配对关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究三腿梯子模型在1/3填充附近的关联电子性质，为理解三层镍酸盐超导体的电子特性提供理论见解。

Method: 采用密度矩阵重整化群方法研究强链间耦合的三腿t-J梯子模型，并对比三腿Hubbard模型中的空穴掺杂态。

Result: 在1/3填充的自旋能隙态中掺杂空穴时，配对关联呈幂律衰减，自旋关联呈指数衰减；而掺杂电子则不会产生显著的配对关联。

Conclusion: 三腿梯子模型中的空穴掺杂态表现出配对特性，这为理解镍酸盐超导体的电子性质提供了重要线索，表明空穴掺杂而非电子掺杂是产生配对关联的关键因素。

Abstract: We investigate the ground-state properties of correlated three-leg ladders near 1/3 filling. We apply the density-matrix renormalization group method to the three-leg t-J ladder with strong interchain couplings and evaluate its pairing nature. When holes are doped into the spin-gapped state at 1/3 filling, we find that pair correlations develop with power-law decays while spin correlations decay exponentially. On the other hand, doping of electrons into the 1/3-filled state does not give rise to substantial pair correlations. We also discuss the hole-doped state in the three-leg Hubbard model to compare it with the pairing state in the t-J model. Our numerical demonstrations provide insights into the electronic properties of trilayer nickelate superconductors.

</details>


### [137] [$0-π$ transitions in non-Hermitian magnetic Josephson junctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17978)
*Roberto Capecelatro,Marco Marciani,Claudio Guarcello,Gabriele Campagnano,Procolo Lucignano,Roberta Citro*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 非厄米磁性约瑟夫森结中的0-π相变受耗散调控，耗散将相变推向更高磁场，且磁场与磁化相对角度也可驱动相变


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非厄米磁性约瑟夫森结的输运特性，探索耗散环境如何影响超导结的0-π相变，揭示非厄米性作为调控超导电流-相位关系的新资源

Method: 采用格林函数计算与限制在亚能隙Andreev准束缚态的有效非厄米描述相结合的方法，研究超导体-量子点-超导体器件耦合铁磁金属库的体系

Result: 耗散将0-π相变推向更高磁场；固定磁场强度下，外加磁场与库磁化相对角度也可驱动相变；这些效应完全归因于非厄米哈密顿量复本征值的行为

Conclusion: 非厄米性可作为工程化超导结电流-相位关系的新控制手段，为超导器件设计提供了额外调控自由度

Abstract: We study the transport properties of non-Hermitian magnetic Josephson junctions, considering a superconductor-quantum dot-superconductor device coupled to a ferromagnetic metallic reservoir in the presence of an external magnetic field. We focus on the $0-π$ transitions that occur when the equilibrium phase difference between the superconductors shifts from $φ=0$ to $φ=π$ upon increasing the magnetic field amplitude. The coupling to the environment induces spin-dependent dissipation and leads to the broadening of the junction Andreev levels. By combining Green's function calculations with an effective non-Hermitian description restricted to the sub-gap Andreev quasi-bound states, we show that dissipation shifts the $0-π$ transition to higher magnetic fields. Remarkably, also the relative angle between the applied field and the reservoir magnetization can be used to drive the transition, at fixed field magnitude. We demonstrate that this effect can be entirely ascribed to the behavior of the complex eigenvalues of the non-Hermitian Hamiltonian. These findings highlight non-Hermiticity as a resource that can introduce new control knobs for engineering the current-phase relation in superconducting junctions.

</details>


### [138] [Superconductivity in Ruddlesden-Popper nickelates: a review of recent progress, focusing on thin films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18385)
*Yang Zhang,Ling-Fang Lin,Thomas A. Maier,Elbio Dagotto*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 综述Ruddlesden-Popper结构镍酸盐超导研究进展，重点关注薄膜体系，比较高压块材与常压薄膜超导特性，探讨未来研究方向


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 镍酸盐超导体的发现为非常规超导研究提供了新平台，但高压条件限制了实验技术应用。常压薄膜超导的发现突破了这一限制，通过比较不同镍氧化物体系的异同，有助于理解强关联电子系统中的高温超导机制

Method: 综述性论文，系统回顾Ruddlesden-Popper镍酸盐的实验和理论进展，特别关注薄膜体系，分析高压块材与常压薄膜超导的相似性和差异性

Result: 总结了双层La3Ni2O7高压超导（Tc~80K）、三层La4Ni3O10高压超导以及La3Ni2O7超薄膜在压缩应变衬底上的常压超导等重要发现，指出常压薄膜超导突破了高压限制，扩展了实验技术应用范围

Conclusion: Ruddlesden-Popper镍酸盐为理解高温超导机制提供了新平台，薄膜体系的发展为实验研究开辟了新途径，未来需要进一步探索不同镍氧化物体系的超导机理和材料设计策略

Abstract: The discovery of superconductivity with Tc ~ 80 K in the nickelate Ruddlesden-Popper bilayer La3Ni2O7 at high pressure has opened a new platform for unconventional superconductivity, followed by the subsequent observation of superconductivity in trilayer La4Ni3O10, also at high pressure. Remarkably, ambient-pressure superconductivity was also observed recently in La3Ni2O7 ultra-thin films when grown on substrates that provide compressive strain. This discovery significantly extends the type of experimental techniques that can be used in nickelates, previously limited due to the high-pressure constraint. Discussing the similarities and differences among these nickel oxides will provide new insights into understanding the mechanism of high-Tc superconductivity in correlated electron systems. In this paper, we review the experimental and theoretical progress on RuddlesdenPopper nickelates, with emphasis on thin films, and discuss future perspectives and research directions.

</details>
