### [139] [Two New Molecular Nitrogen Phases near Megabar Pressures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16641)
*Alexander F. Goncharov,Elena Bykova,Iskander Batyrev,Maxim Bykov,Huawei Chen,William Palfey,Mahmood Mohammad,Stella Chariton,Vitali Prakapenka,Jesse S. Smith*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在78-98 GPa压力下激光加热ζ-N₂至1800-2500 K，合成了两种新的氮分子相：tζ-N₂（C2/c多型，c轴三倍，96原子/晶胞）和ξ-N₂（六方P6cc，112原子/晶胞）


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 氮在聚合转变附近表现出显著的结构多样性，存在多个亚稳态相。研究高压高温条件下氮的新分子相有助于理解其相变行为和结构演化

Method: 使用金刚石压砧在78-98 GPa压力下激光加热ζ-N₂至1800-2500 K合成新相；通过单晶X射线衍射确定晶体结构；结合拉曼光谱验证；第一性原理计算提供理论支持

Result: 成功合成了两种新的氮分子相：tζ-N₂（单斜C2/c多型，c轴三倍，96原子/晶胞）和ξ-N₂（六方P6cc，112原子/晶胞）。tζ-N₂很可能对应先前报道的κ-N₂相

Conclusion: 在高压高温条件下发现了氮的两种新分子相，扩展了对氮在聚合转变附近结构多样性的认识，为理解氮的高压相变提供了新实验证据

Abstract: Molecular nitrogen exhibits remarkable structural diversity near the polymeric transition, where multiple phases are metastable. Here, we report two new molecular phases. The first, $tζ$-N$_2$, is a polytype of monoclinic $C2/c$ $ζ$-N$_2$, characterized by a tripled $c$ axis and 96 atoms per unit cell. The second, $ξ$-N$_2$, is a previously unreported hexagonal phase ($P6cc$) containing 112 atoms per unit cell. Both phases were synthesized in a diamond anvil cell by laser heating $ζ$-N$_2$ to 1800--2500~K at pressures of 78--98~GPa. Their crystal structures were determined using single-crystal X-ray diffraction, corroborated by Raman spectroscopy, and supported by first-principles calculations. The $tζ$-N$_2$ phase likely corresponds to the previously reported $κ$-N$_2$ phase.

</details>


### [140] [Intrinsic grain-size gradients upon grain growth near a free surface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16740)
*Jing Tang,Runlu Yan,Donglan Zhang,Ronald Schnitzer,Lorenz Romaner,Marlene Kapp,Marco Salvalaglio,Oliver Renk*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 研究揭示了自由表面对多晶镍晶粒生长动力学的影响，发现晶粒尺寸从表面到内部存在梯度分布，这种分布受自由表面弹性弛豫和剪切耦合晶界迁移产生的内应力场影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为晶粒生长主要受加权曲率流控制，但最近研究表明弹性效应（由剪切耦合引起的内应力）也需要考虑。本研究旨在探究自由表面对多晶材料晶粒生长动力学的影响，特别是在高纯度块体多晶镍中。

Method: 通过分析1mm厚样品从表面到内部的横截面微观结构演化，以及不同厚度样品（1mm、40μm和10μm）的面内研究，识别晶粒尺寸梯度。研究关注热沟效应可忽略的深度区域（五到十层晶粒深度）。

Result: 发现了固有的晶粒尺寸梯度，表现为晶粒尺寸从表面向内部逐渐增大。这种梯度不仅限于表面，而是延伸到热沟效应可忽略的深度（五到十层晶粒）。研究表明这种行为受自由表面弹性弛豫的显著影响，弹性弛豫改变了由剪切耦合晶界迁移产生的内应力场。

Conclusion: 自由表面对多晶材料的微观结构演化具有重要影响，弹性弛豫通过改变剪切耦合晶界迁移产生的内应力场来影响晶粒生长动力学。这一发现强调了在理解多晶材料微观结构演化时需要考虑自由表面的作用。

Abstract: Grain growth fundamentally shapes the microstructure of crystalline materials upon annealing, affecting their overall mechanical and functional properties. Recently, it has been rationalized that grain growth in polycrystals does not result solely from weighted curvature flow, but elastic effects (intrinsic stress) arised from shear coupling also need to be taken into account. We characterize and examine the effect of free surfaces on grain growth kinetics of high-purity, bulk polycrystalline nickel. By analyzing the microstructural evolution on cross sections of 1 mm thick specimens from the surface to the interior, as well as through in-plane investigations on specimens with varying thickness (1 mm, 40 $μ$m, and 10 $μ$m), an intrinsic grain-size gradient was identified, characterized by a gradual increase in grain size towards the interior. Interestingly, this grading was not restricted to the very surface but continued to depths of five to ten layers of grains, where effects from thermal grooves are considered negligible. We demonstrate that this behavior is significantly affected by elastic relaxation at the free surface, which alters the internal stress fields generated by shear-coupled grain boundary migration. These findings emphasize the relevance of free surfaces to the microstructural evolution of polycrystal.

</details>


### [141] [On the complementary roles of anisotropic crack density and anisotropic crack driving force in phase-field modeling of mixed-mode fracture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16828)
*Guk Heon Kim,Minseo Kim,Kwangsan Chun,Jaemin Kim*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过系统参数分析揭示了各向异性相场断裂模型中两种机制（裂纹密度各向异性和各向异性应变能）的独立作用与协同效应，明确了各自对裂纹路径、断裂韧性和驱动力的控制机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然Pranavi等人提出了各向异性相场断裂的统一框架，但其中两种关键机制（裂纹密度各向异性和各向异性应变能）的具体作用及其相互作用尚未得到深入研究。本研究旨在填补这一空白，通过实验验证和系统参数分析来阐明这两种机制在断裂过程中的独立作用和协同效应。

Method: 首先对软弹性体混合模式断裂实验进行验证，然后对单边缺口(SEN)和开孔拉伸(OHT)试样进行系统参数研究，分别隔离两种机制的作用。通过对比分析裂纹密度各向异性和各向异性应变能在不同几何条件下的表现，揭示它们的独立作用和协同效应。

Result: SEN研究表明：裂纹密度各向异性控制裂纹路径和韧性但不改变弹性响应；各向异性应变能使裂纹偏转但快速饱和。OHT研究表明：各向异性应变能控制纤维取向相关的刚度、峰值力和断裂位移。当两种机制共同作用时，表现出超过线性叠加的非线性协同相互作用。

Conclusion: 裂纹密度各向异性主要控制裂纹路径（断裂阻力），而各向异性应变能控制驱动力，在应力集中几何中额外控制应力集中器周围的弹性应变能分布。这两种机制在断裂过程中具有明确的分工和协同效应。

Abstract: Phase-field models for anisotropic fracture employ two complementary mechanisms: (i) the anisotropic crack density function, controlling direction-dependent fracture resistance, and (ii) the anisotropic strain energy, governing the fracture driving force. Although the unified framework was presented in Pranavi et al.[Comput. Mech., 73 (2024)], the distinct roles of these mechanisms and their interaction remain uninvestigated. This work addresses this gap by first validating the formulation against mixed-mode fracture experiments on a soft elastomer (Lu et al. [Extreme Mech. Lett., 48 (2021)]), and then conducting systematic parametric studies on single-edge-notched (SEN) and open-hole tension (OHT) specimens to isolate each mechanism. The SEN studies show that the crack density anisotropy controls the crack path and toughness while leaving the elastic response unchanged, whereas the anisotropic strain energy deflects the crack but saturates rapidly. The OHT studies reveal a geometry-dependent role expansion: the anisotropic strain energy governs fiber-orientation-dependent stiffness, peak force, and fracture displacement. When both mechanisms act together, the combined response exhibits nonlinear synergistic interaction exceeding the linear sum of the individual contributions. These results establish that the crack density anisotropy governs the crack path (fracture resistance), while the anisotropic strain energy governs the driving force and, in stress-concentration geometries, additionally controls the elastic strain energy distribution around the stress concentrator.

</details>


### [142] [Quantum Computing of Phonon Spectra and Thermal Properties of Crystalline Solids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16863)
*Naman Khandelwal,Bikash K. Behera,Ashok Kumar,Prasanta K. Panigrahi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文将变分量子算法应用于声子哈密顿量，计算了硅和石墨烯的晶格振动和热力学性质，为近量子设备提供了严格的物理基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分量子算法在电子结构计算之外的应用相对较少被探索，需要建立具有物理透明度的严格基准来评估近量子设备上的变分量子算法。

Method: 使用变分量子本征求解器和变分量子降阶法处理从第一性原理力常数导出的声子哈密顿量，将质量加权动力学矩阵映射到量子比特编码的厄米算符，采用误差缓解策略。

Result: 成功计算了硅和石墨烯的完整声子谱，并进一步评估了振动熵、定容比热和热膨胀系数，再现了低温量子行为和高温Dulong-Petit极限，误差缓解策略有助于恢复预期的物理趋势。

Conclusion: 尽管经典声子方法在计算上仍占优势，但该研究将基于声子的热力学确立为评估近量子设备上变分量子算法的严格且物理透明的基准。

Abstract: Variational quantum algorithms offer a promising framework for solving eigenvalue problems on near-term quantum hardware, yet their applicability beyond electronic structure calculations remains relatively unexplored. In this work, we investigate the quantum computing of lattice vibrational and thermodynamical properties by applying the variational quantum eigensolver and variational quantum deflation to phonon Hamiltonians derived from first-principles force constants obtained using density functional theory. The mass-weighted dynamical matrix is mapped onto a qubit-encoded Hermitian operator, enabling computation of the full set of acoustic and optical phonon branches of crystalline silicon and graphene using a reduced qubit register and direct benchmarking against classical diagonalization. The quantum-computed phonon spectrum is further used to evaluate vibrational entropy, constant-volume specific heat, and thermal expansion coefficient, reproducing expected low-temperature quantum behavior and the high-temperature Dulong-Petit limit. We further demonstrate that combined error mitigation strategies help recover phonon dispersions and thermodynamic behavior consistent with expected trends on near-term quantum hardware. Although classical phonon methods remain computationally superior, our results establish phonon-based thermodynamics as a stringent and physically transparent benchmark for assessing variational quantum algorithms on near-term quantum devices.

</details>


### [143] [Crystal Anisotropy Implications on the Magneto-Optical Properties of van der Waals FePS3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16912)
*Ellenor Geraffy,Kusha Sharma,Shahar Zuri,Faris Horani,Adam K. Budniak,Muhamed Dawod,Yaron Amouyal,Thomas Brumme,Andrea Maricel León,Thomas Heine,Rajesh Kumar,Doron Naveh,Efrat Lifshitz*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: FePS3单晶的面内结构各向异性显著影响其光学响应，从体材料到单层极限均观察到四个发射带，具有不同的偏振行为，DFT计算揭示了结构-光学关系的直接联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究FePS3中面内结构各向异性如何影响其光学响应，从体材料到单层极限，建立结构-光学关系，为自旋相关器件应用提供基础理解。

Method: 采用X射线衍射分析体FePS3晶体结构，微光致发光观测体材料和单层FePS3的光学发射，结合密度泛函理论计算从体到单层的电子结构。

Result: 发现四个发射带：一个d-d跃迁（~1.24 eV）和三个p-d电荷转移跃迁（~1.79 eV, ~2.3 eV, ~2.56 eV），这些带表现出不同的偏振行为并持续到单层极限，DFT计算揭示了电子结构和偏振响应的起源。

Conclusion: 建立了FePS3中直接的结构-光学关系，强调了晶格各向异性、电子跃迁和对称性选择光学选择规则之间的强耦合，为自旋电子学应用提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Antiferromagnetic FePS3 has recently gained significant interest in its potential applications in spin-related devices. Here, we show that in-plane structural anisotropy has a major impact in shaping the optical responses of FePS3 single-crystals from the bulk form down to the monolayer limit. X-ray diffraction on a bulk FePS3 crystal confirms a distorted FeS6 octahedron causing inequivalent Fe-Fe distances and consequently resulting in a higher a/b lattice parameter ratio. Micro-photoluminescence observations on bulk and monolayer FePS3 reveal four emissions: one intra-atomic d-d transition (band A, centered at ~1.24 eV) and three p-d charge transfer transitions (bands B, C, and D, centered around ~1.79 eV, ~2.3 eV, and ~2.56 eV, respectively). These bands exhibit different polarization behaviors, which persist down to the monolayer limit. Density functional theory calculations from bulk to monolayer FePS3 reveal the underlying electronic structure, assign the observed emissions, and indicate why these peaks have contrasting linear and circular polarization responses. These results establish a direct structure-optics relation in FePS3, highlighting the strong coupling between lattice anisotropy, electronic transitions, and symmetry-selective optical selection rules.

</details>


### [144] [The Origin of Linearly-Polarized Photoluminescence in WS2/WSe2 Moiré superlattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16934)
*Yuto Urano,Ryo Tamura,Yui Tamogami,Toshikaze Kariyado,Yasumitsu Miyata,Daichi Kozawa,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Ryo Kitaura*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 应变是控制TMD莫尔超晶格中线性偏振光致发光的主要因素，而非谷选择规则


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究WS2/WSe2莫尔激子光学偏振特性的物理机制，特别是线性偏振光致发光对激发偏振不敏感的现象，探索其背后的控制参数

Method: 采用低温下的自动偏振分辨光致发光和拉曼映射技术，结合线性回归分析，通过应变相关描述符预测观测到的偏振特性

Result: 线性偏振度与局部拉曼位移和莫尔激子观测值强相关，应变是主要的实验相关因素；应变相关描述符能最好地预测观测到的偏振

Conclusion: 应变通过放大莫尔势中C3对称性破缺，产生局部椭圆发射的部分抵消，从而产生远场有限线性偏振度，确立了应变作为TMD莫尔超晶格中可靠光学读出关键控制参数的地位

Abstract: Reliable optical control of valley degrees of freedom in moire excitons requires that the emitted polarization faithfully reflect the underlying valley state. Here, we show that linearly polarized photoluminescence from WS2/WSe2 moiré excitons is largely insensitive to the excitation polarization and therefore is not primarily governed by valley-contrast selection rules. Automated polarization-resolved photoluminescence and Raman mapping at cryogenic temperatures reveal that the degree of linear polarization correlates strongly with local Raman shifts and moiré-exciton observables, identifying strain as the dominant experimental correlate. Exhaustive linear-regression analysis further shows that strain-related descriptors provide the best prediction of the observed polarization. Guided by theory, we attribute this behavior to strain-amplified breaking of C3 symmetry in the moire potential: weak uniaxial strain produces only partial cancellation of locally elliptical emission, yielding a finite far-field degree of linear polarization. These results establish strain as a key control parameter for reliable optical readout in TMD moire superlattices.

</details>


### [145] [On the Energy Dissipation in the Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16938)
*Kutay Kulbak,Mohamed Iyad Boualem,Charlie Masse,Mariana Delalibera de Toledo,Vasily V. Temnov*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 研究铁磁纳米磁体在稳定平衡附近的磁化动力学，分析FMR频率、阻尼常数和品质因子Q对自由能极小值局部曲率的依赖关系，特别关注分岔点附近的FMR衰减时间行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究铁磁纳米磁体在Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert框架下的磁化动力学，特别关注在分岔点附近传统品质因子近似Q≈1/2α失效的情况，需要系统分析FMR参数对自由能极小值局部曲率的依赖关系。

Method: 在Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert框架下，通过分析小角度进动，系统研究铁磁共振频率、阻尼常数和品质因子Q对自由能极小值局部曲率的依赖关系，特别关注分岔点附近的行为。

Result: 建立了FMR参数与自由能极小值局部曲率的系统关系，发现在分岔点附近传统近似Q≈1/2α失效，FMR衰减时间表现出特殊行为，需要更精确的描述。

Conclusion: 铁磁纳米磁体的FMR特性强烈依赖于自由能极小值的局部曲率，在分岔点附近传统品质因子近似不再适用，需要更精确的理论描述来理解这些区域的磁化动力学行为。

Abstract: The dynamics of magnetization near a stable equilibrium in ferromagnetic nanomagnets are examined within the Landau--Lifshitz--Gilbert (LLG) framework. For a small angle precession, the dependence of ferromagnetic resonance (FMR) frequency, the damping constant and the resulting quality factor $Q$ of the resonance on the local curvature around the free-energy minimum is systematically analyzed. Special attention is devoted to the behavior of the FMR decay time in the vicinity of bifurcation points, where the number of metastable energy minima changes and the commonly used approximation for the quality factor $Q\simeq 1/2α$ (where $α$ denotes the Gilbert damping) fails.

</details>


### [146] [Hierarchical spectral inhomogeneity in photoluminescence of a twisted MoSe2/WSe2 heterobilayer moiré superlattice revealed by hyperspectral mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16939)
*Nurul Fariha Ahmad,Yuto Urano,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Daichi Kozawa,Ryo Kitaura*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过高光谱光致发光映射和无峰分解光谱分析，揭示了MoSe2/WSe2莫尔超晶格光致发光光谱的层次化不均匀性组织原则


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoSe2/WSe2莫尔超晶格的低温光致发光光谱通常包含宽泛的层间发射背景和密集的窄峰，使得微观的逐线分配变得困难，需要新的分析方法来理解光谱复杂性的空间组织方式

Method: 采用高光谱光致发光映射和无峰分解光谱分析技术，获取20×20的400nm间距映射，进行特征空间相关分析和全光谱相似性分析

Result: 发现三个主要光谱家族形成连续实空间域，特征空间相关分析显示特征微米尺度长度为1.27-2.05μm，超过0.85μm光学光斑尺寸；同时单个像素保留密集多峰结构，表明存在未解析的局部光谱流形；微米尺度能量景观和局部流形复杂性可统计分离但仍相关

Conclusion: 研究确立了层次化不均匀性作为MoSe2/WSe2莫尔超晶格光致发光光谱的组织原则，揭示了光谱复杂性的空间层次结构

Abstract: Low-temperature photoluminescence from MoSe2/WSe2 moiré superlattice often consists of a broad interlayer emission background with dense, narrow peaks, making microscopic line-by-line assignment difficult. Here, we use hyperspectral photoluminescence mapping and peak-decomposition-free spectral analyses to determine how this spectral complexity is organized in space. A 20 x 20 map acquired with a 400 nm pitch reveals three dominant spectral families that form contiguous real-space domains. Feature-wise spatial correlation analysis and whole-spectrum similarity yield a characteristic micron-scale length of 1.27-2.05 um, all exceeding the 0.85 um optical spot size. At the same time, individual pixels retain a dense, multi-peak structure, implying an unresolved local spectral manifold below optical resolution. Correlations among centroid, dominant energy, asymmetry, width, entropy, sharp fraction, and roughness indicate that the micron-scale energy landscape and local manifold complexity can be statistically separated, while remaining correlated across the map, consistent with a hierarchical organization of the emission spectrum. These results establish hierarchical inhomogeneity as an organizing principle of MoSe2/WSe2 moiré superlattice photoluminescence.

</details>


### [147] [Medium-Throughput Evaluation of Transport and Optical Responses in Altermagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17071)
*Fu Li,Bo Zhao,Vikrant Chaudhary,Shengqiao Wang,Chen Shen,Hao Wang,Hongbin Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 开发高通量第一性原理工作流，评估约150种已知反铁磁化合物的输运和光学性质，发现对称性约束下的异常霍尔效应、磁光克尔效应和体光伏效应等响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反铁磁材料在非常规输运和光学响应方面具有超越传统铁磁和反铁磁材料的潜力，但缺乏系统性研究。需要建立系统方法来评估这些材料的输运和光学性质，以识别实验可观测的指纹和功能特性。

Method: 结合密度泛函理论、Wannier插值和对称性分析，开发中等通量第一性原理工作流。从MAGNDATA数据库收集约150种已知反铁磁化合物，研究代表性线性和非线性响应，包括异常霍尔效应、磁光克尔效应和体光伏效应。

Result: 发现这些响应受磁对称性强烈约束，并进一步受自旋轨道耦合、能带结构和反演对称性破缺影响。具体发现：金属VNb3S6存在有限异常霍尔响应，绝缘体CaIrO3具有巨大克尔旋转，非中心对称CuFeS2产生大位移电流。

Conclusion: 这些结果为识别反铁磁材料中实验可观测的指纹和功能输运性质建立了对称性指导的路径，为反铁磁材料在非常规输运和光学应用中的潜力提供了系统评估框架。

Abstract: Altermagnets provide a promising platform for unconventional transport and optical responses beyond conventional ferromagnets and antiferromagnets. In this work, we develop a medium-throughput first-principles workflow to evaluate transport and optical properties in approximately 150 known altermagnetic compounds collected from the MAGNDATA database. By combining density functional theory, Wannier interpolation, and symmetry analysis, we investigate representative linear and nonlinear responses, including the anomalous Hall effect, magneto-optical Kerr effect, and bulk photovoltaic effect. We find that these responses are strongly constrained by magnetic symmetry and further shaped by spin-orbit coupling, band structure, and inversion symmetry breaking. Representative examples include a finite anomalous Hall response in metallic VNb3S6, giant Kerr rotation in insulating CaIrO3, and large shift current in non-centrosymmetric CuFeS2. These results establish a symmetry-guided route for identifying experimentally accessible fingerprints and functional transport properties in altermagnetic materials.

</details>


### [148] [Unraveling the significance of Raman modes, Gruneisen parameters and phonon lifetimes in the hexagonal allotropes of Silicon and Germanium compounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17204)
*Lekshmi S M,Upasana Agrawal,Akarsh Jain,Siddharth Sastri,Suvadip Das*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文采用第一性原理密度泛函理论，系统研究了两种材料的电子结构、振动性质、声子输运和热力学特性，为热电、光伏和光电器件应用提供优化策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子信息技术的发展推动了功能性量子材料的探索，需要深入理解材料的电子结构和振动性质，以优化其在热电、光伏和光电器件中的应用性能。

Method: 采用第一性原理密度泛函理论（DFT）和密度泛函微扰理论（DFPT），使用meta-GGA泛函分析电子结构和振动性质，研究拉曼活性声子模式、声子寿命、声子-声子散射、三声子散射率、声子线宽，以及频率和温度依赖的Grüneisen参数。

Result: 系统分析了两种材料的声子寿命、散射速率和线宽，研究了温度依赖的热膨胀和热导率，揭示了声子谱中的非谐性效应，为材料性能调控提供了理论基础。

Conclusion: 通过深入理解材料的电子结构和振动性质，提出了调控材料性能的策略，旨在提高其在先进热电、光伏和光电器件应用中的效能。

Abstract: Advancement in quantum information and quantum technologies has ushered in a new era of technological revolution in large scale atomistic simulation and efficient system on a chip device fabrication. This has led to innovative ways of harnessing rigorous search algorithms for functional quantum materials and steered scientists to dig deeper into the world of quantum phenomenon and applications. In this work, we delineate the advanced electronic structure and vibrational properties utilizing the popular meta-GGA functionals, spectral signatures of the Raman active phonon modes, explored their average mean free paths, and whether they conserve helicity, by leveraging first principles density functional theory and density functional perturbation theory. A systematic analysis of the role of phonon lifetimes, consequences of phonon-phonon and three phonon scattering rates and phonon linewidths have been presented. Further, a study of the the frequency and temperature dependent Gruneisen parameter has been employed in conjecture with the temperature dependent thermal expansion and thermal conductivity to portray the effect of anharmonicity in the phonon spectra of these two materials. Finally, we provide strategies for tuning the properties of these materials in an effort to improve their efficacy for advanced thermoelectric, photovoltaic and optoelectronic device applications.

</details>


### [149] [Influence of Ni substitution on the phase transitions and magnetocaloric effect of NdCo2 at cryogenic temperatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17336)
*Vilde G. S. Lunde,Øystein S. Fjellvåg,Allan M. Döring,Marc Straßheim,Vladimir Pomjakushin,Konstantin P. Skokov,Oliver Gutfleisch,Tino Gottschall,Joachim Wosnitza,Anja O. Sjåstad,Bjørn C. Hauback,Christoph Frommen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 研究NdCo2-xNix立方Laves化合物中Ni部分替代Co对相变和磁热效应的影响，发现Ni含量增加会降低相变温度、抑制正交相并减小磁矩，导致磁热效应减弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究部分Ni替代Co对NdCo2立方Laves化合物的相变行为和磁热效应的影响，探索通过化学替代调控材料磁热性能的途径。

Method: 采用中子衍射和体磁化测量研究NdCo2-xNix化合物（0≤x≤1），通过间接和直接方法测定磁热效应，分析Ni替代对相变温度和磁矩的影响。

Result: Ni含量增加使立方-四方相变（100K→降低）和四方-正交相变（42K→降低）温度下降；x≥0.5时正交相被抑制；Ni替代导致磁矩减小；NdCo2在20T磁场下绝热温变为6.3K，NdCoNi降至4.9K。

Conclusion: Ni部分替代Co能有效调控NdCo2的相变行为和磁热性能，但会降低磁矩和磁热效应强度，为磁热材料设计提供了化学替代策略。

Abstract: We have investigated NdCo2-xNix cubic Laves compounds with 0 <= x <= 1 using neutron diffraction and bulk magnetization measurements to study the influence of partial Ni substitutions of Co on the phase transitions and the magnetocaloric effect. Upon cooling, NdCo2 undergoes a cubic to tetragonal transition at 100 K, and a tetragonal to orthorhombic transition at 42 K. The transitions are associated with long-range ferromagnetic ordering of the magnetic moments along the c axis and spin reorientation into the ab plane, respectively. Both transitions shift to lower temperatures as the Ni content x increases. For x >= 0.5, the orthorhombic phase is suppressed. Additionally, there was a reduction in the magnetic moment upon increasing the Ni substitution of Co. The magnetocaloric effect was determined both indirectly and directly, with good agreement between the methods. NdCo2 exhibits an adiabatic temperature change of 6.3 K for a field of 20 T, which is decreased to 4.9 K for NdCoNi for the same field strength due to the reduced magnetic moment upon Ni substitution.

</details>


### [150] [G-type antiferromagnetic structure in Rb1-xV2Te2O](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17365)
*Wu Xie,Changchao Liu,Fayuan Zhang,Zhenhong Tan,Wenhai Ji,Nan Zhao,Lingxiang Bao,Dong Zhang,Feiran Shen,Lunhua He,Hao Wang,Rong Du,Guanghan Cao,Chaoyu Chen,Ping Miao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过中子粉末衍射研究发现Rb1-xV2Te2O在337K以下呈现G型反铁磁结构，与理论预期不同，为理解该候选交变磁体提供了新视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交变磁体因其非相对论性自旋分裂能带结构和补偿磁矩特性受到广泛关注。发现具有实用特性的新型交变磁体材料是重要研究任务。Rb1-xV2Te2O作为金属室温交变磁体候选材料已有实验报道，但其磁结构需要进一步验证。

Method: 采用中子粉末衍射技术研究Rb1-xV2Te2O的磁结构，确定其磁性有序状态和相变温度。

Result: 研究发现Rb1-xV2Te2O在337K以下呈现G型反铁磁结构，这一结果与原始理论预期不同，表明该材料的磁结构特性需要重新认识。

Conclusion: 中子粉末衍射揭示了Rb1-xV2Te2O的G型反铁磁结构，与理论预期存在差异，这一发现可能为该交变磁体候选材料的物理机制提供新的理解方向。

Abstract: Altermagnetism, known for its non-relativistic spin-split band structures with yet compensated moments, is being intensively investigated. Discovering new altermagnetic materials with characteristics suitable for practical use remains an important ongoing task. Recently a metallic room-temperature altermagnet candidate Rb1-xV2Te2O with a layered structure and d-wave spin symmetry has been reported based on experimental results from the spin-resolved photoemission spectroscopy and scanning tunnelling microscopy/spectroscopy (STM/STS) measurements. Here we report neutron powder diffraction (NPD) investigations on the magnetic structure of Rb1-xV2Te2O, which shows a G-type antiferromagnetic structure below the transition temperature of 337 K. The result is different from the original theoretical expectation, which might lead to new insights on the physics of this altermagnet candidate.

</details>


### [151] [Modern Solid Electrolytes for All-Solid-State Batteries: Materials Chemistry, Structure, and Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17380)
*Denys Butenko,Mustafa Khan,Liusuo Wu,Jinlong Zhu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该综述从晶体对称性到非晶态局部多面体排列，系统分析了无机固态电解质（氧化物、硫化物、卤化物）的结构-性能关系，强调离子传导取决于框架拓扑、缺陷化学、瓶颈响应等多尺度因素的耦合效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨无机固态电解质中结构-性能关系的理论基础，通过比较氧化物、硫化物和卤化物三种主要化学体系，揭示影响快速离子传导的关键结构因素，为设计兼具高导电性、稳定性和加工性的新型电解质提供指导。

Method: 采用比较分析方法，从晶体对称性、局部多面体排列、缺陷化学等角度，系统考察氧化物、硫化物和卤化物固态电解质的结构特征。结合实验和计算手段，建立多尺度结构-性能关系，分析框架拓扑、位点能量分布、瓶颈响应等对离子传输的影响机制。

Result: 氧化物在化学稳定但几何受限的框架中实现离子传输；硫化物通过软而易极化的晶格拓宽低能迁移路径；卤化物处于中间状态，其紧密堆积的阴离子亚晶格、近似简并的锂环境和混合阴离子配位实现有效传输，同时增强氧化稳定性和正极兼容性。离子传输被理解为统计连接的局部低势垒迁移事件的宏观结果。

Conclusion: 快速离子传导不仅取决于组成或晶体扩散路径，而是框架拓扑、缺陷化学、瓶颈响应等多因素耦合的结果。未来电解质设计需综合考虑导电性、稳定性和加工性的协同优化，建立多尺度结构-性能关系的研究方法至关重要。

Abstract: In this review, from crystallographic symmetry to amorphous local polyhedra arrangement and combinations, we examine inorganic solid state electrolytes through the lens of structure property relationships, with oxides, sulfides, and halides representing three major framework chemistries. Halide solid electrolytes and their derivatives, including mixed anion halides and antiperovskite related materials, have expanded this landscape further by introducing new ways to regulate local coordination chemistry, defect populations, and transport active frameworks. Across these families, fast ion conduction depends not simply on composition or crystallographic diffusion pathways, but on the coupled effects of framework topology, site energy distribution, defect chemistry, bottleneck response, and local anion flexibility. Oxides illustrate transport within chemically robust but geometrically constrained frameworks. Sulfides demonstrate that a soft, easily polarizable lattice can broaden the array of low energy migration pathways. Halides occupy an intermediate state, in which the closely packed anion sublattices, an approximately degenerate lithium environment, and mixed anion coordination enable effective transport while simultaneously enhancing oxidation stability and compatibility with cathodes. Building on these comparisons, we argue that long range ion transport is increasingly understood not as motion along a single idealized pathway, but as the macroscopic outcome of statistically connected low barrier local migration events distributed across the structure. We further discuss the experimental and computational approaches required to establish such multiscale structure property relationships and outline future strategies for designing transport active frameworks in which conductivity, stability, and processability are optimized together.

</details>


### [152] [Designer metal-free altermagnetism in honeycomb two-dimensional frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17386)
*Hongde Yu,Thomas Brumme,Thomas Heine*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种在二维有机晶体中实现金属自由反铁磁性的分子设计策略，通过降低三角烯衍生物自由基的单体点群对称性，选择性破坏反演对称性同时保持二分晶格，实现了d波自旋分裂和反铁磁耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反铁磁性结合了相反自旋通道的动量依赖自旋分裂和零净磁化，能够实现对外磁场鲁棒的自旋输运电场控制。虽然在无机系统中广泛探索，但在二维有机框架中具有π自旋分裂的金属自由反铁磁体仍然难以实现。

Method: 通过将三角烯衍生物自由基的单体点群对称性从D3h降低到C2v，选择性破坏反演对称性同时保持二分晶格。采用自旋极化密度泛函理论计算揭示强反铁磁耦合、M点的d波自旋分裂和Mott-Hubbard带隙。构建最小紧束缚模型分析各向异性最近邻跳跃的微观起源。

Result: 计算显示强反铁磁耦合为-130 meV，M点的d波自旋分裂为17 meV，Mott-Hubbard带隙为1.26 eV，完全符合Lieb定理。各向异性最近邻跳跃是自旋分裂和反铁磁性的微观起源。双轴压缩应变可将自旋分裂增强至27 meV。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了一种实现室温有机反铁磁体的通用方法，通过工程化反演对称性破坏开辟了基于碳的反铁磁性途径，为碳基自旋电子学提供了新方向。

Abstract: Altermagnetism combines momentum-dependent spin splitting of opposite-spin channels with zero net magnetization, enabling electric-field control of spin transport that is robust against external magnetic fields. Although widely explored in inorganic systems, metal-free altermagnets with pi-spin splitting, particularly in two-dimensional organic frameworks, have remained elusive. Here, we introduce a molecular design strategy that achieves designer metal-free altermagnetism in honeycomb 2D crystals. By reducing the monomer point-group symmetry from D3h to C2v in triangulene-derived radicals, inversion symmetry is selectively broken while the bipartite lattice is preserved. Spin-polarized density-functional-theory calculations reveal strong antiferromagnetic couplings of -130 meV, d-wave spin splitting of 17 meV at the M point, and Mott-Hubbard gaps of 1.26 eV, all fully consistent with Lieb's theorem. A minimal tight-binding model shows that anisotropic nearest-neighbor hopping arising from direction-dependent pi-orbital overlap is the microscopic origin of spin splitting and altermagnetism. Biaxial compressive strain further enhances the spin splitting to 27 meV. These results establish a general approach to room-temperature organic altermagnets and open a pathway toward carbon-based altermagnetism via engineered inversion-symmetry breaking.

</details>


### [153] [Localized Exciton Emission with Spontaneous Circular Polarization in NiPS3/WSe2 Heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17409)
*Adi Harchol,Shahar Zuri,Rajesh Kumar Yadav,Nirman Chakraborty,Idan Cohen,Tomasz Woźniak,Thomas Brumme,Thomas Heine,Doron Naveh,Efrat Lifshitz*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: NiPS3/WSe2二维范德华异质结中通过界面诱导势场产生局域激子，在无外磁场下出现自发圆偏振，表明存在磁性邻近效应，为手性光源和磁可调光电器件提供新平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索二维范德华异质结中通过界面邻近效应调控电子、光学和磁学性质的可能性，特别是结合二维半导体与层状反铁磁体来实现对谷极化和自旋自由度的控制。

Method: 采用低温微区光致发光（μ-PL）和磁光致发光（magneto-PL）光谱技术研究NiPS3/WSe2异质结的光学响应，结合密度泛函理论（DFT）计算验证实验结果并揭示界面机制。

Result: 异质结中出现多个尖锐激子峰，这些峰在单一材料中不存在，表明界面诱导势场产生了局域化的层内WSe2激子。激子在无外磁场下表现出自发圆偏振，证实磁性邻近效应。磁光测量显示非线性塞曼分裂，表明界面交换场改变了谷激子动力学。DFT计算确认了光致发光的层内起源，并揭示了界面杂化和自旋织构修饰。

Conclusion: 二维半导体与层状反铁磁体的结合能够有效调控谷极化和自旋自由度，为手性光源和磁可调光电器件开辟了新途径，展示了范德华异质结在自旋电子学和谷电子学应用中的潜力。

Abstract: Two-dimensional (2D) van der Waals (vdW) heterostructures (HSs) provide a versatile platform for tailoring electronic, optical, and magnetic properties via proximity effects at their interfaces. In this work, we explore the optical response of few-layer NiPS3/WSe2 HSs using low-temperature micro-photoluminescence (μ-PL) and magneto-PL spectroscopy. The HSs exhibit multiple sharp excitonic peaks that do not appear in the individual constituent materials, indicating the emergence of localized intralayer WSe2 excitons confined by interface-induced potentials. Notably, these excitons exhibit spontaneous circular polarization even in the absence of an external magnetic field, suggesting a magnetic proximity effect induced by uncompensated spins at the NiPS3 interface. Magneto-PL measurements further reveal nonlinear Zeeman splitting, consistent with the presence of an interfacial exchange field that alters the valley exciton dynamics. Density functional theory (DFT) calculations confirm the intralayer origin of the PL and reveal interfacial hybridization and spin texture modifications, supporting the experimental findings. These results highlight how combining a 2D semiconductor with a layered antiferromagnet enables control over valley polarization and spin degrees of freedom, offering new opportunities for chiral light sources and magnetically tunable optoelectronic devices.

</details>


### [154] [Spin State versus Potential of Zero Charge as Predictors of Density-Dependent Oxygen Reduction in M-N-C Electrocatalysts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17427)
*Di Zhang,Zixun Yu,Fangzhou Liu,Yumeng Li,Jiaxiang Chen,Xun Geng,Yuan Chen,Li Wei,Hao Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究发现，在M-N-C电催化剂中，金属位点密度对氧还原活性和选择性的影响主要通过零电荷电位（PZC）而非自旋态来预测。PZC随密度的变化改变了界面电场，从而调节ORR中间体的吸附能，解释了实验观察到的密度依赖性趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金属位点密度显著影响M-N-C电催化剂的氧还原活性和选择性，但预测这些趋势的描述符仍存在争议。研究旨在比较自旋态和零电荷电位作为密度依赖性氧还原行为的预测因子，以确定更有效的描述符。

Method: 采用约束磁化计算结合Landau分析研究基态磁矩变化；通过显式溶剂模拟分析PZC随密度的变化；将PZC变化纳入pH-电场耦合微动力学模型；进行实验PZC测量验证预测趋势。

Result: 基态磁矩在广泛的金属位点密度范围内变化微弱，表明磁描述符无法解释显著的性能变化。PZC呈现系统性密度依赖性偏移，改变了界面电场并调节ORR中间体的吸附能。微动力学模型成功捕捉了密度依赖性活性趋势，并再现了酸性条件下低密度时双电子选择性增加的实验现象。实验PZC测量支持预测趋势。

Conclusion: PZC比自旋态更能有效预测M-N-C电催化剂中密度依赖性的氧还原活性和选择性。PZC通过改变界面电场调节ORR中间体吸附能，是理解密度依赖性性能变化的关键描述符。

Abstract: Metal-site density strongly influences oxygen reduction activity and selectivity in M-N-C electrocatalysts, but the descriptors that predict these trends remain under debate. Here, we compare spin state and the potential of zero charge as predictors of density-dependent oxygen reduction behavior in Fe-N-C and Co-N-C catalysts. Using constrained-magnetization calculations combined with Landau analysis, we find that the ground-state magnetic moments vary only weakly across a broad range of metal-site densities, suggesting that magnetic descriptors alone cannot account for the pronounced performance changes. In contrast, explicit-solvent simulations reveal systematic density-dependent shifts in PZC, which alter the interfacial electric field and thereby modulate field-sensitive adsorption energetics of ORR intermediates. Incorporating these PZC shifts into a pH-field-coupled microkinetic model captures the density-dependent activity trends and reproduces the experimentally observed increase in two-electron selectivity at lower site densities under acidic conditions. Experimental PZC measurements further support the predicted trend. Together, these results show that PZC is a more effective predictor than spin state for density-dependent oxygen reduction activity and selectivity in M-N-C electrocatalysts.

</details>


### [155] [Crystallographic Challenges in Microscopy of Multidomain Spinel Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17561)
*Ninon Scherz,Shashwat Anand,Colin Ophus,Tucker Holstun,Mary Scott,Tara P. Mishra,Gerbrand Ceder*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文评估了STEM-HAADF成像和傅里叶滤波在表征δ-DRX尖晶石域及其反相边界中的应用，揭示了特定取向下某些边界不可检测的现象，并指出看似无序区域可能源于倾斜的低能域边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原子分辨电子显微技术已常规用于研究高性能锰基氧化物阴极的微观结构，这些材料常含有尖晶石类有序结构。需要评估STEM-HAADF成像和傅里叶滤波作为工具来表征δ-DRX尖晶石域及其反相边界，这些边界对材料电化学性能起关键作用。

Method: 使用电子显微模拟和δ-DRX结构组成的最新理论见解，尝试表征其多域结构中出现的晶体学尖晶石变体。通过STEM-HAADF成像和傅里叶滤波分析，研究八个不同变体配对产生的域界面。

Result: 每个域界面（来自八个不同变体的配对）可分为四种傅里叶滤波轮廓之一，其中一种在沿优选[110]带轴观察时，在原子分辨电子显微中使边界不可检测。结果还表明，看似无序或层状区域的表象可能源于相对于[110]观察方向倾斜的低能域边界。

Conclusion: 研究结果强调了在相变的原子分辨率显微图像解释中需要格外谨慎，其中局部重排驱动从高对称性到低对称性结构的转变，同时保持晶格相干性。傅里叶滤波分析有助于揭示这些复杂域结构。

Abstract: Electron microscopy techniques are instrumental in the characterization of energy storage materials, with atomic resolution images providing the detailed structural features that are needed to understand their properties. Atomically resolved electron microscopy techniques have been routinely used to study the microstructure in high performing Mn-based oxide cathodes, which often contain spinel-like ordering. Here, we evaluate STEM-HAADF imaging and subsequent Fourier filtering as tools for characterizing δ-DRX spinel domains and their antiphase boundaries, which play a central role in the material's electrochemical performance. Using electron microscopy simulations and recent theoretical insight into the structural makeup of δ-DRX, we attempt to characterize the crystallographic spinel variants which occur in its multi-domain structure. We show that each domain interface, arising from pairings among eight distinct variants, can be categorized into one of four Fourier filtered profiles, one of which leaves the boundary undetectable in atomically resolved electron microscopy when viewed along the preferred [110] zone axis. Our results also suggest that the appearance of seemingly disordered or layered-like regions might actually arise from low energy domain boundaries which are slanted relative to the [110] viewing direction. Our findings highlight the need for careful interpretation of atomic-resolution micrographs of phase transitions, where local reordering drives transformations from higher to lower symmetry structures while maintaining lattice coherence.

</details>


### [156] [Seed Layer Engineering for Effective Charge Transfer Doping of MoS$_2$ Transistors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17729)
*Sahej Sharma,Shao-Heng Yang,Himani Jawa,Rana Yuvraj,Bach Nguyen,Chang Niu,Shiva Radhakrishnan,Shalini Tripathi,Dennis Lin,Cesar Javier Lockhart de la Rosa,Pierre Morin,Dmitry Zemlyanov,Francesca Iacopi,Zhihong Chen,Joerg Appenzeller,Thomas E. Beechem*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了种子层在MoS₂晶体管中的双重作用：不仅促进介电层成核和粘附，还通过引入无序和调控界面电荷转移来显著影响器件性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将二维半导体（如MoS₂）与介电材料集成是未来逻辑技术应用的核心挑战。虽然通常引入种子层来促进介电层成核和粘附，但其对电荷转移掺杂和晶体管性能的调控作用尚未充分理解。

Method: 制备了背栅单层MoS₂晶体管，顶部采用Ta种子层/HfOₓ介电堆叠进行钝化。通过电学表征（阈值电压、导通电流）和多种物理表征技术（拉曼光谱、光致发光光谱、X射线光电子能谱）系统分析不同Ta种子层厚度和沉积条件对器件性能的影响。

Result: 阈值电压和导通电流随Ta种子层厚度和沉积条件显著变化。光学光谱显示导通电流与种子层引入的无序相关，XPS表明阈值电压与界面电荷转移引起的局部静电环境变化相关。在贫氧条件下沉积的超薄0.2 nm Ta种子层表现出最佳性能，既能限制沉积损伤又能促进电荷转移。

Conclusion: 种子层工程是控制MoS₂器件中无序和界面掺杂的关键策略，多模态光谱学可作为制造过程中的实用方法，用于工艺开发和监控。

Abstract: Integrating two-dimensional semiconductors such as MoS$_2$ with dielectric materials remains a central challenge for their use in future logic technologies. While seed layers are typically introduced to promote dielectric nucleation and adhesion, we show that they also critically govern charge-transfer doping and, in turn, transistor performance. Back-gated monolayer MoS$_2$ transistors passivated on their top-surface with a Ta-seed/HfO$_x$ dielectric stack were fabricated and characterized electrically and physically using Raman, photoluminescence, and X-ray photoelectron spectroscopies. Threshold voltage and on-current varied strongly with Ta-seed thickness and deposition conditions, and these changes correlated with signatures observed across all spectroscopic probes. The results reveal that the seed layer both introduces disorder into the MoS$_2$ channel and modifies the interfacial charge environment controlling charge transfer between HfO$_x$ and MoS$_2$. Optical spectroscopy shows that on-current tracks seed-induced disorder, whereas X-ray photoelectron spectroscopy indicates that threshold voltage correlates with shifts in the local electrostatic environment associated with interfacial charge transfer. Better performance was obtained with ultrathin 0.2 nm Ta seed layers deposited under oxygen-poor conditions, which limit deposition-induced damage while facilitating charge transfer. These findings identify seed-layer engineering as a key strategy for controlling disorder and interfacial doping in MoS$_2$ devices and establish multimodal spectroscopy as a practical during-fabrication approach for process development and monitoring.

</details>


### [157] [Three-dimensional visualization of lattice defects in $β$-Ga$_2$O$_3$ via synchrotron-radiation Borrmann-effect X-ray topo-tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17826)
*Yongzhao Yao,Daiki Katsube,Hirotaka Yamaguchi,Shinya Yamaguchi,Daiki Wakimoto,Hironobu Miyamoto,Yukari Ishikawa*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 首次在β-Ga2O3中实现位错的三维可视化，利用同步辐射X射线拓扑断层扫描技术，在双束Borrmann效应条件下获得深度分辨的位错图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: β-Ga2O3是下一代功率电子器件的有前途材料，但其性能受位错等晶格缺陷的强烈影响，需要开发能够三维可视化这些缺陷的方法以理解其对器件性能的影响。

Method: 采用同步辐射X射线拓扑断层扫描技术，在透射X射线形貌术中利用双束Borrmann效应条件。通过绕衍射矢量旋转样品并在不同旋转角度获取一系列拓扑断层图像，捕获位错对比度的演化，实现深度分辨的位错可视化。

Result: 成功实现了β-Ga2O3中位错的三维重建，能够清晰区分肖特基势垒二极管结构中外延层和衬底的位错，为理解位错传播及其对外延生长和器件性能的影响提供了直观洞察。

Conclusion: 这是首次在β-Ga2O3中实现位错三维重建的研究，该方法为分析晶格缺陷及其对器件性能的影响提供了强有力的工具，有助于优化β-Ga2O3基功率电子器件的生长和性能。

Abstract: beta-Ga2O3 is a promising material for next-generation power electronics; however, its performance is strongly affected by lattice defects such as dislocations. In this study, we demonstrate three-dimensional (3D) visualization of dislocations in \b{eta}-Ga2O3 using synchrotron-radiation X-ray topo-tomography under a two-beam Borrmann-effect condition in transmission X-ray topography. By rotating the sample about the diffraction vector and acquiring a series of topo-tomographic images at different rotation angles, the evolution of dislocation contrast is captured, providing intuitive, depth-resolved visualization of dislocations. This method enables clear separation of dislocations in the epilayer and substrate in Schottky barrier diode structures, offering insight into dislocation propagation and their impact on epitaxial growth and device performance. This study represents the first demonstration of 3D dislocation reconstruction in beta-Ga2O3.

</details>


### [158] [Multipolar Piezoelectricity and Anisotropic Surface Transport in Alterelectrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18324)
*Amber Visser,Viktor Könye,Oleg Janson,Jeroen van den Brink,Corentin Coulais,Jasper van Wezel*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 论文提出"alterelectrics"作为altermagnets的电学对应物，具有四极压电性和双曲色散，表面模式实现各向异性电子输运


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: altermagnets结合了铁磁和反铁磁特性，具有自旋分离能带但净磁化强度为零，其对称性导致四极压磁性和双曲色散。需要区分哪些性质本质源于磁性，哪些由对称性决定。

Method: 引入基于电极化的"alterelectrics"作为altermagnets的替代方案，通过简化模型和第一性原理材料实现展示其四极压电性和双曲色散特性。

Result: alterelectrics表现出四极压电性和双曲色散，其表面模式形成类似于自旋分离能带的对应物，可实现表面依赖的各向异性电子输运，类似于altermagnets的自旋电子学应用。

Conclusion: 通过引入alterelectrics概念，成功区分了altermagnets中源于磁性的性质与由对称性决定的性质，为各向异性电子输运提供了新的电学实现途径。

Abstract: Altermagnets are an emergent class of materials combining features of ferro- and antiferro-magnetic materials. They have spin-separated bands normally associated with ferromagnets, but a vanishing net magnetization. Moreover the symmetries giving rise to $d$-wave altermagnetism can provide them with a particular anisotropic, quadrupolar (i.e. with equal and opposite values when strained in perpendicular directions) piezomagnetism. Observing that the same symmetries provide a natural place to look for hyberbolic wave dispersion, this raises the question which properties are intrinsically linked to magnetism and which are determined by the symmetry. Here, we disentangle these concepts by introducing an alternative to altermagnets, based on electric polarization. These alterelectrics display quadrupolar piezoelectricity and a hyperbolic dispersion, which we demonstrate conceptually within a simplified model as well as a first-principles material realization. We furthermore establish that a counterpart of the spin-separated bands is formed by surface modes which allow for surface dependent anisotropic electronic transport analogous to the spintronic applications proposed for altermagnets.

</details>


### [159] [Type-II-like ultrafast demagnetization behavior in NiCo2O4 thin films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17916)
*Ryunosuke Takahashi,Kaede Yamada,Harjinder Singh,Kanata Watanabe,Junta Igarashi,Julius Hohlfeld,Jon Gorchon,Gregory Malinowski,Daisuke Kan,Yuichi Shimakawa,Takayuki Ishibashi,Stephane Mangin,Hiroki Wadati*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文研究了NiCo2O4薄膜的超快退磁动力学，发现其具有两步退磁行为：实验时间分辨率内的即时信号降低，以及约5-6 ps的特征时间尺度的较慢退磁分量，随后在~100 ps时间尺度上恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀土-亚铁磁氧化物是研究超快自旋动力学的有前景平台。本文旨在研究NiCo2O4薄膜的光致磁化动力学，探索其超快磁响应特性，特别是多亚晶格自旋动力学。

Method: 采用时间分辨磁光法拉第效应，使用两种独立的泵浦-探测配置：1030/515 nm和800/400 nm。通过这两种配置研究NiCo2O4外延薄膜的光致磁化动力学。

Result: 在两种测量中，光激发均引起磁光信号的即时降低（在实验时间分辨率内），随后出现可重复的较慢退磁分量（特征时间尺度约5-6 ps），并在~100 ps时间尺度上恢复。皮秒退磁分量在两种实验配置和激发波长下均一致观察到，表明这是NCO薄膜超快磁响应的固有特征。

Conclusion: 研究确立了NiCo2O4中稳健的两步超快退磁行为，并强调无稀土氧化物亚铁磁体是探索超快时间尺度多亚晶格自旋动力学的有前景系统。由于最早时间点的信号下降可能包含瞬态光学贡献，作者将整体响应描述为"类II型"，而非基于亚分辨率信号直接赋予明确的教科书式II型分类。

Abstract: Rare-earth-ferrimagnetic oxides are emerging as attractive platforms for investigating ultrafast spin dynamics. Here, we study the photoinduced magnetization dynamics of epitaxial NiCo2O4 (NCO) thin films by time-resolved magneto-optical Faraday effect using two independent pump-probe configurations: 1030/515 nm and 800/400 nm. In both measurements, photoexcitation induces an immediate reduction of the magneto-optical signal within the experimental time resolution, followed by a reproducible slower demagnetization component with a characteristic timescale of approximately 5-6 ps and a subsequent recovery on the ~100 ps timescale. Importantly, this picosecond demagnetization component is observed consistently across the two experimental configurations and excitation wavelengths, demonstrating that it is an intrinsic feature of the ultrafast magnetic response of NCO thin films. Because the earliest-time dip may contain a transient optical contribution, we describe the overall response as type-II-like, rather than assigning a definitive textbook type-II classification solely on the basis of the sub-resolution signal. These results establish a robust two-step ultrafast demagnetization behavior in NCO and highlight rare-earth-free oxide ferrimagnets as promising systems for exploring Mult sublattice spin dynamics on ultrafast timescales.

</details>


### [160] [SWORD: Symmetry and Wyckoff-sequence of Ordered and Disordered crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17994)
*Yuyao Huang,Wei Nong,Shuya Yamazaki,Martin Hoffmann Petersen,Jianghai Wang,Ruiming Zhu,Kedar Hippalgaonkar*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: SWORD是一种对称感知的Wyckoff基字符串表示方法，用于有序和无序晶体，提供标准化结构描述、显式表示部分占据位点、量化复杂无序，实现高效结构分组和去重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料发现中的新颖性要求候选材料具有独特性、非冗余性和热力学合理性。随着晶体学数据库规模和复杂度的增加，特别是涉及晶体学无序时，部分占据位点极大地扩大了结构-组成空间，使得有效可靠的新颖性评估变得困难。

Method: 引入SWORD（对称感知Wyckoff基字符串表示），该方法：1）将对称等效结构描述标准化为一致标签；2）显式表示部分占据位点上的共占据物种；3）通过混合度描述符量化复杂无序，捕捉位点化学计量的连续变化。

Result: SWORD在保持身份保持变换不变性的同时，对结构扰动保持可解释的敏感性。在关联未弛豫和中间构型与其最终弛豫状态方面表现出竞争力，可用于直接从部分弛豫甚至未弛豫的生成结构进行可靠新颖性评估。成功应用于ICSD数据库的去重和整理。

Conclusion: SWORD为有序和无序晶体提供了一种有效的结构表示方法，能够实现数据库规模的无序感知去重和整理，为人工智能时代材料信息学和数据驱动材料设计提供基础。

Abstract: Novelty in materials discovery requires candidates to be distinct, non-redundant, and thermodynamically plausible. While crystallographic databases continue to expand in both size and complexity, making efficient and reliable novelty assessment has become increasingly difficult. This becomes particularly acute when crystallographic disorder is involved, as partial occupancies greatly enlarge the structure-composition space and obscure the identification of genuinely distinct structures. Here, we introduce SWORD, a symmetry-aware, Wyckoff-based string representation compatible with both ordered and disordered crystals. SWORD provides (i) standardization of symmetry-equivalent structural descriptions into a consistent label, (ii) explicitly represents co-occupying species on partially occupied sites, and (iii) quantifies complex disorder through a degree of mixing descriptor that captures continuous variation in site stoichiometry. These features enable efficient structure grouping, duplicate identification, and finer refinement of disordered structures. Benchmarking against existing fingerprint and structure-matching methods shows that SWORD remains invariant under identity-preserving transformations while retaining interpretable sensitivity to structural perturbations. In addition, SWORD shows competitive performance in associating unrelaxed and intermediate configurations with their final relaxed states along relaxation trajectories. This feature could enable more reliable novelty assessment directly from partially relaxed or even unrelaxed generated structures. Finally, SWORD was used to showcase its capability of disorder-aware database-scale deduplication and curation for the Inorganic Crystal Structure Database (ICSD). The curated ICSD would serve as the basis for the materials informatics and data-driven materials design in the era of artificial intelligence.

</details>


### [161] [Polarization Engineering of the Orbital Hall Conductivity in Two-dimensional Ferroelectric Higher-Order Topological Insulator Tl$_2$S and SnS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18093)
*YingJie Hu,Heng Gao,Yabei Wu,Wei Ren*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了二维铁电高阶拓扑绝缘体中极化方向与轨道霍尔电导的耦合机制，发现面内极化可作为可逆开关调控带隙内的轨道输运平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索铁电极化与高阶拓扑绝缘体之间的相互作用，理解极化方向如何调控拓扑性质和轨道输运行为，为可控轨道电子学提供新途径。

Method: 研究两类具有面外或面内极化的二维铁电高阶拓扑绝缘体，分析其拓扑起源和轨道输运行为，以Tl₂S和SnS作为代表性模型进行理论计算和机制分析。

Result: 发现面内极化与高阶拓扑之间存在强耦合，面内极化可作为固有手段可逆地切换带隙内的轨道霍尔电导平台；Tl₂S展示持久性轨道输运，SnS展示电可切换轨道输运。

Conclusion: 该研究深化了对铁电性、高阶拓扑和轨道输运之间耦合机制的理解，为开发可控轨道电子器件提供了新思路，特别是利用面内极化作为轨道输运的可逆开关。

Abstract: Ferroelectric higher-order topological insulators (HOTIs) exhibit tunable physical properties arising from the interplay between ferroelectric polarization and band topology. This work investigates the topological origin of two classes of two-dimensional (2D) ferroelectric HOTIs with out-of-plane or in-plane polarization, revealing their distinct orbital transport behaviors and the mechanism for engineering orbital Hall conductivity (OHC) via polarization control. Our results demonstrate the unique role of polarization in modulating both the higher-order band topology and orbital transport. A strong coupling between in-plane polarization and higher-order topology is identified, establishing in-plane polarization as an intrinsic means to reversibly switch the OHC plateau within the band gap. Using Tl$_2$S and SnS as representative models of the two HOTI types, we demonstrate persistent and electrically switchable orbital transport, respectively. Our study advances the understanding of the coupling among ferroelectricity, higher-order topology, and orbital transport, offering new avenues for controllable orbitronics.

</details>


### [162] [Evaluating dispersion models for ab initio simulation of G-I and G-II molten fluoride salts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18139)
*Shubhojit Banerjee,Rajni Chahal Crockett,Julian Barra,Stephen T Lam*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究系统评估了DFT-D和vdW-DF色散修正对熔融氟化物（LiF、NaF、KF、BeF₂、MgF₂、CaF₂）结构、热物理和输运性质预测的影响，发现色散修正对密度预测影响显著但对扩散系数影响较小，为熔盐模拟中色散模型选择提供了系统框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准DFT交换相关泛函常忽略色散相互作用，导致熔盐性质预测误差。虽然常采用色散修正来匹配实验密度，但其对熔盐结构、热物理和输运性质的系统性影响尚未得到充分研究，特别是在反应堆应用相关的熔融氟化物中。

Method: 使用基于密度泛函理论的从头算分子动力学（AIMD），评估Grimme的DFT-D和非局域范德华（vdW-DF）色散修正对第I族（LiF、NaF、KF）和第II族（BeF₂、MgF₂、CaF₂）熔融氟化物的影响。通过系统基准测试比较不同组成和温度下的性质预测。

Result: 色散修正对结合能影响较小，但对密度预测影响显著。半经验色散模型通常比vdW-DF产生更准确的密度。在固定密度下，扩散系数基本不受色散修正影响，但配位数分布随色散模型选择呈现明显差异。BeF₂表现出与其他氟化物不同的行为，在没有色散修正时显示出明显的结构和动力学差异。

Conclusion: 色散效应对促进中长程有序的高电荷密度阳离子（如Be²⁺）至关重要。研究结果为熔盐模拟中选择色散模型提供了系统框架，能改善密度和结构预测的准确性。

Abstract: Ab initio molecular dynamics (AIMD) based on density functional theory (DFT) is a powerful approach for modeling molten salts. However, standard exchange-correlation functionals often neglect dispersion interactions, introducing potential errors in property predictions. Dispersion corrections are commonly applied ad hoc to match experimental salt densities, but their systematic impact on predicting structure, thermophysical, and transport properties of salt remains unexamined. This study evaluates the impact of Grimme's DFT-D and nonlocal van der Waals (vdW-DF) corrections on molten fluorides of Group-I (LiF, NaF, KF) and Group-II (BeF$_2$, MgF$_2$, CaF$_2$), which are relevant to reactor applications. Results indicate that dispersion corrections have a minor effect on binding energies but significantly influence density predictions. Systematic benchmarking across compositions and temperatures reveals that semi-empirical dispersion models often produce more accurate densities compared to vdW-DF. Diffusion coefficients remain largely invariant to dispersion corrections at fixed densities, while coordination number distributions exhibit notable differences based on chosen dispersion. BeF$_2$, in particular, deviates from other fluorides, showing pronounced structural and dynamical differences in the absence of dispersion corrections. This highlights the necessity of dispersion effects for high-charge-density cations that promote intermediate- to long-range ordering. These findings provide a systematic framework for selecting dispersion models in molten salt simulations, improving density and structural predictions.

</details>


### [163] [Direct observation of quadruple spin-texture locking in a 2D d-wave altermagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18337)
*Dan Mu,Bei Jiang,Qingchen Duan,Zulin Xu,Xingkai Cheng,Yusen Xiao,Xinru Han,Xinyu Liang,Zhaokun Luo,Ryan L. Kong,Qiheng Wang,Junwei Liu,Jianxin Zhong,Ruidan Zhong,Qiangqiang Gu,Baiqing Lv,Hong Ding*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究首次在原子尺度上直接观测到RbV2Se2O中的d波型自旋织构，证实了自旋-晶格锁定现象，并发现了新的自旋-条纹锁定机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交变磁体具有零净磁化强度和动量依赖的自旋分裂特性，为自旋电子学提供了新范式。自旋-晶格锁定是交变磁体的核心机制，但原子尺度的直接观测证据一直缺失，阻碍了对交变磁体基本性质的理解。

Method: 使用原位场可切换的自旋极化Cr针尖，在相同能量下实现准粒子干涉的自旋对比映射。通过分析RbV2Se2O中的自旋选择性散射响应和干涉图案，揭示自旋织构特征。

Result: 首次在原子尺度观测到d波型自旋织构，证实了自旋-晶格锁定。发现干涉图案表现出明显的自旋依赖性调制，建立了自旋散射锁定和自旋动量锁定。意外发现自旋选择性散射响应由长周期条纹调制组织，揭示了新的自旋-条纹锁定现象，归因于自旋密度波莫尔图案的出现。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了d波交变磁体中四重自旋织构锁定现象的统一图像，将交变磁体定位为探索自旋、晶格、动量、莫尔势和谷等多自由度相互作用的通用平台。

Abstract: Altermagnets combine vanishing net magnetization with nonrelativistic, momentum-dependent spin splitting, offering a new paradigm for spintronics. Spin-crystal symmetry coupling, namely spin-lattice locking, is the defining mechanism of altermagnetism, enforcing opposite spin sublattices in real space and spin-momentum-locked electronic structure in reciprocal space. Direct atomic-scale visualization of spin-lattice locking therefore constitutes a decisive benchmark of the altermagnetic state, yet such evidence has remained elusive despite extensive efforts. Here we show that the electronic states in RbV2Se2O exhibit a d-wave-like spin texture at the sublattice level, providing the first atomic-scale evidence of spin-lattice locking with a predominantly c-axis spin orientation. By employing an in-situ, field-switchable spin-polarized Cr tip, we realize spin-contrast mapping of quasiparticle interference at identical energies, overcoming a long-standing experimental barrier in altermagnets. The resulting interference patterns exhibit pronounced spin-dependent modulations, establishing spin scattering locking and spin momentum locking as the real and reciprocal space manifestations. Unexpectedly, we uncover that the spin-selective scattering response is organized by a long-period stripe modulation, giving rise to a previously unidentified form of spin-texture locking, spin-stripe locking. We attribute this behavior to the emergence of a spin-density-wave moiré pattern. Together, these results establish a unified picture of quadruple spin-texture locking phenomena in a d-wave altermagnet, and position altermagnets as a versatile platform for exploring many-body interactions among intertwined degrees of freedom, including spin, lattice, momentum, moiré potential and valley.

</details>


### [164] [Plasmonic Photocatalysis Enables Selective Oxidative Coupling of Methane with Nitrous Oxide under Ambient Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18417)
*Serin Lee,Lin Yuan,Elijah Begin,Dali Yang,Cedric Lim,Yirui Arlene Zhang,Lu Ma,Colin Ophus,Yi Cui,Junwei Lucas Bao,Jennifer A. Dionne*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究开发了一种等离子体光催化剂，可在常温常压下将甲烷和氧化亚氮转化为C2-C3烃类，选择性约80%，抑制了CO2生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 甲烷和氧化亚氮是强效温室气体，传统转化方法需要高温（高达1000°C），产生大量CO2排放且对多碳产物选择性有限。需要开发在温和条件下高效选择性转化的方法。

Method: 通过系统调控AuPd合金在TiO2上的组成，确定最优配比(AuPd0.05)，其中Au增强光捕获，Pd促进选择性C-H活化和C-C偶联。结合原位光谱、热载流子计算和从头算方法研究反应机理。

Result: 在可见光照射下，催化剂产生C2H4、C2H6、C3H6和C3H8，选择性约80%，同时抑制CO2形成。等离子体产生的载流子重新分布界面羟基中间体，将亲水中心转移以抑制过度氧化。光照下C-C偶联能垒从2.7eV降至0.7eV。

Conclusion: 通过调控界面电子和吸附物动力学，可以实现选择性多碳产物形成，为温室气体在温和条件下转化为高价值化学品提供了新途径。

Abstract: Methane (CH4) and nitrous oxide (N2O) are potent greenhouse gases that represent substantial chemical energy. Conversion of these abundant waste gases to high-value chemicals typically requires high temperatures up to 1000 C, producing substantial CO2 emissions and limited selectivity toward desirable multi-carbon products. Here we demonstrate a plasmonic photocatalyst that enables CH4 and N2O conversion under ambient conditions to form C2 and C3 hydrocarbons. By systematically tuning AuPd alloys on TiO2, we identify an optimal composition (AuPd0.05) where Au enhances light harvesting and Pd enables selective C-H activation and C-C coupling. Under visible-light illumination, this catalyst produces C2H4, C2H6, C3H6, and C3H8 with ~80% selectivity while suppressing CO2 formation. In-situ spectroscopy and hot-carrier calculations show that plasmon-generated carriers redistribute interfacial hydroxyl intermediates, shifting the hydrophilic center to suppress overoxidation. Ab-initio calculations further reveal the reduction in C-C coupling barriers from 2.7 eV to 0.7 eV under illumination. Our work illustrates how engineering interfacial electronic and adsorbate dynamics enables selective multicarbon formation.

</details>


### [165] [Moire Control of Alterelectric Quadrupolar Order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18428)
*Alejandro Lopez-Bezanilla*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文展示了莫尔超晶格可以将alterelectric（补偿铁电）四极矩有序转变为可控相，通过莫尔场调控四极矩内部取向，实现可编程各向异性电子功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究如何将alterelectric（补偿铁电）这种无净极化的四极矩电子有序转变为可控相，探索莫尔超晶格作为实现可编程各向异性电子功能的通用途径。

Method: 采用布洛赫周期双轨道理论，将缓慢变化的层间配准粗粒化为作用于自洽双分量alterelectric四极矩的有效莫尔场，通过配准相扫描提供四极矩空间内的连续路径。

Result: 莫尔超晶格不仅稳定了alterelectric有序，还能调控其内部取向；相变发生在强烈依赖于填充的不稳定性阈值之上，从弱选择区域过渡到强轴向主导基态；取向选择直接编码在莫尔布里渊区内低能谱权重的重新分布中。

Conclusion: 莫尔超晶格是实现可控alterelectric有序和可编程各向异性电子功能的通用途径，能够调控四极矩的内部取向，为新型电子器件设计提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Alterelectricity is a compensated ferroic state in which quadrupolar electronic order reshapes low-energy electronic structure without producing a net polarization. Here we show that a moiré superlattice can turn such order into a controllable phase. Within a Bloch-periodic two-orbital theory, the slowly varying interlayer registry is coarse-grained into an effective moiré field acting on a self-consistent two-component alterelectric quadrupole. The resulting phase develops above a strongly filling-dependent instability threshold and crosses over from a weakly selected regime into a robust axial-dominated ground state, while the diagonal-dominated branch remains only a weak competitor. A registry-phase sweep supplies an explicit continuous path through internal quadrupole space, demonstrating that the moiré superlattice does more than stabilize alterelectricity: it steers its internal orientation. This orientational selection is encoded directly in the redistribution of low-energy spectral weight across the moiré Brillouin zone. These results identify moiré superlattices as a generic route to controllable alterelectric order and to programmable anisotropic electronic functionality.

</details>
